#### `lemma Roth.mem_support_taylor_iff`

### `theorem Roth.mem_support_taylor_iff`
- **Type**: `∀ {σ : Type*} {P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ} {α : σ → ℝ} {d : σ →₀ ℕ}, d ∈ (taylor α P).support ↔ eval α (hasseDeriv d P) ≠ 0`
- **What**: A multi-index d lies in the support of the Taylor shift P(X + α) iff the d-th Hasse derivative of P does not vanish at α; i.e. the support of the shifted polynomial is exactly the set of tuples entering the minimum that defines the index.
- **How**: `rw [mem_support_iff, coeff_taylor]` — mathlib's `MvPolynomial.mem_support_iff` turns membership into `coeff d (taylor α P) ≠ 0`, and the project's Taylor theorem `MvPolynomial.coeff_taylor` (Roth/Taylor.lean:134) rewrites the coefficient as `eval α (hasseDeriv d P)`.
- **Hypotheses**: none beyond the implicit data; `omit [Fintype σ] in` drops the section's `Fintype σ`. Stated over ℝ only because the surrounding file is; the proof works verbatim over any `CommSemiring R` (both rewritten lemmas are stated at that generality).
- **Uses from project**: `MvPolynomial.taylor` (Roth/Taylor.lean:42), `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv` (Roth/HasseDeriv.lean), `MvPolynomial.coeff_taylor` (Roth/Taylor.lean:134)
- **Used by**: Roth/Index.lean only (`grep -rn mem_support_taylor_iff Roth/`): `index_le_weight` (l.72, `.2`), `exists_weight_eq_index` (l.81, `.1`), `le_index_iff` (l.90, `.1`), `index_le_card` (l.228, `.2`). Transitively everything built on these index API lemmas.
- **Visibility**: private
- **Lines**: 61–64 (incl. `omit` line), proof length 1 line
- **Notes**: Declared `private lemma` (kind recorded as theorem). The same rewrite pair is inlined once elsewhere, in `degreeOf_taylor_le` (Roth/Taylor.lean:173, `simp [mem_support_iff, coeff_taylor, ...]`), which is in a different file and cannot see this private lemma.

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.mem_support_taylor_iff.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.mem_support_taylor_iff.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.index_le_weight`

### `theorem Roth.index_le_weight`
- **Type**: `{P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ} {α : σ → ℝ} {r : σ → ℕ} {d : σ →₀ ℕ} (h : eval α (hasseDeriv d P) ≠ 0) : index P α r ≤ weight r d` (with `[Fintype σ]`)
- **What**: The index of `P` at `α` with weights `r` is at most the weight `∑ d_j / r_j` of any multi-index `d` whose Hasse derivative `Δ_d P` does not vanish at `α`.
- **How**: `csInf_le` applied to the image `weight r '' (taylor α P).support`, which is bounded below because it is finite (`Finset.finite_toSet`, `Set.Finite.image`, `Set.Finite.bddBelow`); membership via `Set.mem_image_of_mem` and the private `mem_support_taylor_iff` (`mem_support_iff` + `coeff_taylor`).
- **Hypotheses**: `h`: the `d`-th Hasse derivative of `P` is nonzero at `α`, i.e. `d` lies in the support of `P(X + α)`.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.index`, `Roth.weight`, `MvPolynomial.taylor`, `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv`, `Roth.mem_support_taylor_iff` (private; via `MvPolynomial.coeff_taylor`)
- **Used by**: Roth/Index.lean:87 (`le_index_iff`), :105 (`index_eq_zero_iff`), :114 (`index_sub_weight_le_index_hasseDeriv`), :153 (`index_eq_weightedOrder_div`), :245 (`index_unique`); Roth/Main.lean:401
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 66–72, proof length 2 lines
- **Notes**: no positivity of `r` needed (the `sInf` def is total in `r`); no `P ≠ 0` needed (vacuous then).

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_le_weight.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_le_weight.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.exists_weight_eq_index`

### `theorem Roth.exists_weight_eq_index`
- **Type**: `{P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ} (hP : P ≠ 0) (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) : ∃ d, eval α (hasseDeriv d P) ≠ 0 ∧ weight r d = index P α r` (with `[Fintype σ]`)
- **What**: For a nonzero polynomial the index is an attained minimum: some multi-index `d` with `(Δ_d P)(α) ≠ 0` has weight `∑ d_j / r_j` exactly equal to `index P α r`.
- **How**: `Set.Nonempty.csInf_mem` applied to the image `weight r '' (taylor α P).support`, which is finite (`Finset.finite_toSet`, `Set.Finite.image`) and nonempty (`simpa using hP`, i.e. `taylor α P ≠ 0`); the support membership is translated by the private `mem_support_taylor_iff` (`mem_support_iff` + `coeff_taylor`).
- **Hypotheses**: `hP : P ≠ 0` (makes the support of the Taylor expansion nonempty; necessary, see evidence (3)).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.index`, `Roth.weight`, `MvPolynomial.taylor`, `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv`, `Roth.mem_support_taylor_iff` (private), and the simp lemma(s) giving `taylor α P = 0 ↔ P = 0` used by `simpa using hP`
- **Used by**: Roth/Index.lean:101 (`index_eq_zero_iff`), :112 (`index_sub_weight_le_index_hasseDeriv`), :226 (`index_le_card`); Roth/MainLemma.lean:457 (`exists_hasseDeriv_eval_ne_zero`)
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 74–81, proof length 5 lines
- **Notes**: Companion of `index_le_weight` (the other half of "index = attained minimum"). No positivity of `r` needed.

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.exists_weight_eq_index.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.exists_weight_eq_index.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.le_index_iff`

### `theorem Roth.le_index_iff`
- **Type**: `{P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ} (hP : P ≠ 0) {α : σ → ℝ} {r : σ → ℕ} {c : ℝ} : c ≤ index P α r ↔ ∀ d, eval α (hasseDeriv d P) ≠ 0 → c ≤ weight r d` (with `[Fintype σ]`)
- **What**: For a nonzero polynomial, a real `c` is at most the index of `P` at `α` with weights `r` iff every multi-index `d` with `(Δ_d P)(α) ≠ 0` has weight `∑ d_j / r_j ≥ c`.
- **How**: forward direction is `h.trans (index_le_weight hd)`; backward direction is `le_csInf` with nonemptiness from `simpa using hP`, then `rintro` over the image and the private `mem_support_taylor_iff` (`mem_support_iff` + `coeff_taylor`).
- **Hypotheses**: `hP : P ≠ 0` (nonempty support of `P(X+α)`; necessary, evidence (3)).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.index`, `Roth.weight`, `Roth.index_le_weight`, `Roth.mem_support_taylor_iff` (private), `MvPolynomial.taylor`, `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv`, and the simp lemma(s) giving `taylor α P = 0 ↔ P = 0` used by `simpa using hP`
- **Used by**: Roth/Index.lean:121 and :125 (`le_index_sum`), :154 (`index_eq_weightedOrder_div`), :248 (`index_unique`); Roth/MainLemma.lean:423 (`le_index_of_forall_eval_eq_zero`)
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 83–90, proof length 4 lines
- **Notes**: listed as a main result in the module docstring. No positivity of `r` needed. Third sibling of `index_le_weight` and `exists_weight_eq_index` in the "index = attained minimum" API.

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.le_index_iff.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.le_index_iff.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.index_nonneg`

### `theorem Roth.index_nonneg`
- **Type**: `(P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) : 0 ≤ index P α r` (with `[Fintype σ]`)
- **What**: The index of any real multivariate polynomial at any point, for any weights, is nonnegative.
- **How**: `Real.sInf_nonneg` applied to `weight r '' supp(taylor α P)`, with every member nonnegative by `Set.forall_mem_image` and the project's `weight_nonneg`.
- **Hypotheses**: none.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.index`, `Roth.weight_nonneg` (Roth/Box.lean:75), implicitly `Roth.weight`, `MvPolynomial.taylor`
- **Used by**: Roth/Index.lean:100 (`index_eq_zero_iff`); Roth/Theta.lean:110 (`Θ_nonneg`), Roth/Theta.lean:395
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 92–94, proof length 1 line
- **Notes**: holds for all `r` (no positivity) and for `P = 0` (junk value `sInf ∅ = 0`).

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_nonneg.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_nonneg.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.index_eq_zero_iff`

### `theorem Roth.index_eq_zero_iff`
- **Type**: `{P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ} (hP : P ≠ 0) {r : σ → ℕ} (hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j) (α : σ → ℝ) : index P α r = 0 ↔ eval α P ≠ 0` (with `[Fintype σ]`)
- **What**: For a nonzero real polynomial and positive weights, the index of `P` at `α` is zero iff `P(α) ≠ 0`.
- **How**: (→) take the attaining multi-index from `exists_weight_eq_index`; its weight is `0`, so `weight_eq_zero_iff hr` gives `d = 0`, and `hasseDeriv_zero_apply` turns `(Δ_0 P)(α) ≠ 0` into `P(α) ≠ 0`. (←) antisymmetry of `index_nonneg` with `index_le_weight` at `d = 0` (weight `0`).
- **Hypotheses**: `hP : P ≠ 0` (otherwise the junk value `index 0 = 0` breaks (→); necessary, evidence (3a)); `hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j` (otherwise `d_j / 0 = 0` gives weight-zero nonzero multi-indices; necessary, evidence (3b)).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.index`, `Roth.exists_weight_eq_index`, `Roth.index_nonneg`, `Roth.index_le_weight`, `Roth.weight_eq_zero_iff` (Roth/Box.lean:91), `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv`, `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv_zero_apply` (Roth/HasseDeriv.lean:112)
- **Used by**: Roth/Index.lean:182 (`index_prod`, empty case: `index 1 α r = 0`)
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 96–106, proof length 7 lines
- **Notes**: listed under Main results in the module docstring; it is the second half of the source's first index lemma (clean.tex l.558–566).

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_eq_zero_iff.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_eq_zero_iff.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.index_sub_weight_le_index_hasseDeriv`

### `theorem Roth.index_sub_weight_le_index_hasseDeriv`
- **Type**: `{σ : Type*} [Fintype σ] {P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ} {k : σ →₀ ℕ} (hk : hasseDeriv k P ≠ 0) (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) : index P α r - weight r k ≤ index (hasseDeriv k P) α r`
- **What**: If `Δ_k P ≠ 0`, then `ind(Δ_k P; α; r) ≥ ind(P; α; r) − ∑_j k_j / r_j`: a Hasse derivative of order `k` lowers the index by at most the weight of `k`.
- **How**: Take `i` attaining the index of `Δ_k P` (`exists_weight_eq_index hk`). By `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv_hasseDeriv`, `Δ_i Δ_k P = binom(i+k, i) · Δ_{i+k} P`, so (via `smul_eq_C_mul`, `eval_C`, `mul_ne_zero_iff`) `(Δ_{i+k} P)(α) ≠ 0`. Then `index_le_weight` gives `ind P ≤ weight r (i+k) = weight r i + weight r k` (`weight_add`), and `sub_le_iff_le_add` concludes.
- **Hypotheses**: `hk : hasseDeriv k P ≠ 0` (necessary: without it the junk value `index 0 = 0` breaks the inequality, evidence (3)); no positivity on `r` needed.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.index`, `Roth.weight`, `Roth.exists_weight_eq_index`, `Roth.index_le_weight`, `Roth.weight_add` (Roth/Box.lean), `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv`, `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv_hasseDeriv` (Roth/HasseDeriv.lean:175)
- **Used by**: Roth/MainLemma.lean:494 (index bound for the derivative `hasseDeriv k P` in the main-lemma construction); Roth/Theta.lean:390 (`hterm`, index of `E μ ν` in the Theta argument); listed in the module docstring of Roth/Index.lean:35.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 107–114, proof length 3 lines
- **Notes**: Lean form of the source's Lemma `lem:index-of-derivative` (clean.tex l.571–588), with the same proof as the source ("choose a multi-index i attaining the index of Δ_k P; the Hasse composition identity …").

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_sub_weight_le_index_hasseDeriv.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_sub_weight_le_index_hasseDeriv.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.le_index_sum`

### `theorem Roth.le_index_sum`
- **Type**: `{ι : Type*} {s : Finset ι} {f : ι → MvPolynomial σ ℝ} {α : σ → ℝ} {r : σ → ℕ} (hs : ∑ i ∈ s, f i ≠ 0) {c : ℝ} (hf : ∀ i ∈ s, f i ≠ 0 → c ≤ index (f i) α r) : c ≤ index (∑ i ∈ s, f i) α r` (with `[Fintype σ]`)
- **What**: If a finite sum of real polynomials is nonzero and every nonzero summand has index at least `c` (at `α`, weights `r`), then the sum has index at least `c`. This is the finite-sum form of `ind(P+Q) ≥ min(ind P, ind Q)` (source Lemma 4-1).
- **How**: `(le_index_iff hs).2`; for `d` with `(Δ_d Σ f_i)(α) ≠ 0`, push `hasseDeriv` and `eval` through the sum with `map_sum`, pick a summand with nonzero value by `Finset.exists_ne_zero_of_sum_ne_zero`, note that summand is nonzero, and conclude with `(le_index_iff hfi).1`.
- **Hypotheses**: `hs` (sum nonzero; necessary because of the junk value `index 0 = 0`, evidence (3)); `hf` (lower bound on nonzero summands only; the guard `f i ≠ 0 →` is a genuine weakening, since a zero summand has index `0`, evidence (4)).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.index`, `Roth.le_index_iff`, `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv` (via `map_sum`, it is a linear map)
- **Used by**: Roth/Theta.lean:398 (bound for the index of the Leibniz expansion of the generalised Wronskian determinant)
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 115–126, proof length 5 lines
- **Notes**: listed under Main results in the module docstring ("the index of sums and products").

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.le_index_sum.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.le_index_sum.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.weight_eq_finsuppWeight_div`

### `theorem Roth.weight_eq_finsuppWeight_div`
- **Type**: `{σ : Type*} [Fintype σ] {r : σ → ℕ} (hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j) (d : σ →₀ ℕ) : weight r d = (Finsupp.weight (fun j ↦ (∏ i, r i) / r j) d : ℝ) / ∏ i, r i`
- **What**: For positive integers `r_j` and `D = ∏ r_j`, the rational weight `∑ d_j / r_j` of a multi-index equals the natural-number weight `∑ d_j · (D / r_j)` (with the natural weights `w_j = D / r_j`) divided by `D`.
- **How**: `hD : 0 < ∏ (r i : ℝ)` by `Finset.prod_pos`; unfold `weight`, rewrite with `Finsupp.weight_eq_sum`, `Nat.cast_sum`, `Finset.sum_div`, `Nat.cast_prod`; per term (`Finset.sum_congr`) use `Nat.cast_mul`, `Nat.cast_div_charZero` with `Finset.dvd_prod_of_mem`, then `mul_div_assoc`, `div_right_comm`, `mul_div_cancel_right₀`.
- **Hypotheses**: `hr` (all `r j` positive; necessary, evidence (3); only nonvanishing is used).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.weight`
- **Used by**: Roth/Index.lean:152 and Roth/Index.lean:155 (both inside `Roth.index_eq_weightedOrder_div`); no other file.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 127–137, proof length 7 lines
- **Notes**: implements the source's Lean note ("set `D = ∏ r_j` and use the natural weights `w_j = D / r_j`", clean.tex l.549–556).

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.weight_eq_finsuppWeight_div.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.weight_eq_finsuppWeight_div.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.index_eq_weightedOrder_div`

### `theorem Roth.index_eq_weightedOrder_div`
- **Type**: `{σ : Type*} [Fintype σ] {P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ} (hP : P ≠ 0) {r : σ → ℕ} (hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j) (α : σ → ℝ) : index P α r = (((taylor α P : MvPowerSeries σ ℝ).weightedOrder fun j ↦ (∏ i, r i) / r j).toNat : ℝ) / ∏ i, r i`
- **What**: For positive integer parameters `r`, the Roth index of `P` at `α` equals the weighted order of the Taylor translate `P(X + α)` (as a power series) with natural weights `w_j = D / r_j`, `D = ∏ r_j`, divided by `D`.
- **How**: Obtains a coefficient `d` attaining the weighted order (`MvPowerSeries.exists_coeff_ne_zero_and_weightedOrder`, finiteness from `MvPowerSeries.ne_zero_iff_weightedOrder_finite`); `≤` by `index_le_weight` at `d` after rewriting `weight` via `weight_eq_finsuppWeight_div`; `≥` by `le_index_iff`, `div_le_div_of_nonneg_right`, `ENat.toNat_le_toNat` and `MvPowerSeries.weightedOrder_le`; coefficients identified via `MvPolynomial.coeff_coe` and `coeff_taylor`.
- **Hypotheses**: `hP : P ≠ 0` (superfluous, evidence (1)); `hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j` (necessary, evidence (2)).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.index`, `MvPolynomial.taylor`, `MvPolynomial.coeff_taylor`, `Roth.weight_eq_finsuppWeight_div`, `Roth.index_le_weight`, `Roth.le_index_iff`, `Roth.weight` (via the rewrite).
- **Used by**: Roth/Index.lean:164, 165 (`index_mul`, three rewrites); mentioned in the module docstring Roth/Index.lean:38. No other file.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 139–158, proof length 13 lines
- **Notes**: Formalises the paper's Lean implementation note (clean.tex §4, "use the natural weights `w_j = D/r_j` in `MvPowerSeries.weightedOrder`, then divide the finite value by `D`"). The `hP` hypothesis is superfluous as stated by the Roth.index report; verified independently here.

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_eq_weightedOrder_div.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_eq_weightedOrder_div.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.index_mul`

### `theorem Roth.index_mul`
- **Type**: `{σ : Type*} [Fintype σ] {P Q : MvPolynomial σ ℝ} (hP : P ≠ 0) (hQ : Q ≠ 0) (α : σ → ℝ) {r : σ → ℕ} (hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j) : index (P * Q) α r = index P α r + index Q α r`
- **What**: For nonzero real polynomials `P`, `Q` and positive integer weights `r`, the Roth index at `α` is additive: `ind(PQ) = ind(P) + ind(Q)`.
- **How**: Rewrites all three indices with `index_eq_weightedOrder_div` (natural weights `w_j = ∏ r / r_j`), merges with `← add_div`, pushes the product through `taylor` (`map_mul`) and the power-series coercion (`MvPolynomial.coe_mul`), applies `MvPowerSeries.weightedOrder_mul`, and closes with `ENat.toNat_add` (finiteness by `simp`, from nonzeroness) and `Nat.cast_add`.
- **Hypotheses**: `hP`, `hQ : _ ≠ 0` (necessary in Lean, evidence (1)); `hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j` (used by the proof through `index_eq_weightedOrder_div`; mathematically expected to be superfluous, not verified, see Phase 4).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.index`, `Roth.index_eq_weightedOrder_div`, `MvPolynomial.taylor` (via `map_mul` on the algebra hom).
- **Used by**: Roth/Index.lean:185 (`index_prod`, insert step); Roth/Theta.lean:438 (Lemma 4-2-style split of an index over a product of renamed polynomials); module docstring Roth/Index.lean:37.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 160–169, proof length 7 lines
- **Notes**: Formalises clean.tex §4 Lemma 4-1 (multiplicative half), following the paper's Lean note ("For integral r_j, apply weightedOrder_mul through translation and the normalisation").

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_mul.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_mul.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.index_prod`

### `theorem Roth.index_prod`
- **Type**: `{σ : Type*} [Fintype σ] {ι : Type*} (s : Finset ι) {f : ι → MvPolynomial σ ℝ} (hf : ∀ i ∈ s, f i ≠ 0) (α : σ → ℝ) {r : σ → ℕ} (hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j) : index (∏ i ∈ s, f i) α r = ∑ i ∈ s, index (f i) α r`
- **What**: For a finite family of nonzero real polynomials and positive integer weights `r`, the Roth index at `α` of the product is the sum of the indices.
- **How**: `Finset.induction_on`. Empty case: `Finset.prod_empty`, `Finset.sum_empty`, then `index_eq_zero_iff one_ne_zero hr α` (index of `1` is `0` since `eval α 1 ≠ 0`). Insert case: `Finset.prod_insert`, `Finset.sum_insert`, `index_mul` (nonzeroness of the tail product via `Finset.prod_ne_zero_iff`) and the induction hypothesis.
- **Hypotheses**: `hf : ∀ i ∈ s, f i ≠ 0` (necessary in Lean, evidence (1)); `hr : ∀ j, 0 < r j` (needed by `index_mul` / `index_eq_zero_iff` / the bridge; mathematically expected superfluous, not verified, same situation as `index_mul`).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.index`, `Roth.index_mul`, `Roth.index_eq_zero_iff`.
- **Used by**: Roth/Theta.lean:407 (index of the product of matrix entries in a determinant term); module docstring Roth/Index.lean:37.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 172–186, proof length 10 lines
- **Notes**: Formalises clean.tex Lemma 4-1 iterated, as used at clean.tex line 825 (proof of Lemma 6).

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_prod.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_prod.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.index_neg`

### `theorem Roth.index_neg`
- **Type**: `{σ : Type*} [Fintype σ] (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) : Roth.index (-P) α r = Roth.index P α r`
- **What**: The index of a real polynomial at `α` with weights `r` is unchanged when the polynomial is negated.
- **How**: `simp [index, support_neg]`: unfold `Roth.index` (an `sInf` of `weight r` over `(taylor α P).support`); `taylor α` is a ring hom so `taylor α (-P) = -taylor α P` (`map_neg`), and `MvPolynomial.support_neg` gives equal supports.
- **Hypotheses**: none beyond the section variable `[Fintype σ]`; no nonvanishing or positivity hypothesis is needed (both sides are `0` for `P = 0`).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.index` (and implicitly `MvPolynomial.taylor` through its unfolding)
- **Used by**: unused in project (`grep -rnw index_neg Roth/` returns only the declaration at Roth/Index.lean:190)
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 189-192 (docstring 189, statement 190-191, proof 192), proof length 1 line
- **Notes**: Docstring cites §5, proof of Lemma 6 ("Multiplication by a sign does not change the index", clean.tex:825). In the formalisation of that step (Roth/Theta.lean:402-406, `sum_max_sub_le_index_det`) the sign is the integer cast `Equiv.Perm.sign s`, and the step is discharged with `Roth.index_C_mul`, not with `index_neg`.

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_neg.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_neg.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.index_C_mul`

### `theorem Roth.index_C_mul`
- **Type**: `{σ : Type*} [Fintype σ] {c : ℝ} (hc : c ≠ 0) (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) : Roth.index (C c * P) α r = Roth.index P α r`
- **What**: Multiplying a real polynomial by a nonzero constant does not change its Roth index at `α` with weights `r`.
- **How**: Unfolds both indices (`sInf` of `weight r` over `(taylor α _).support`). `map_mul` and `MvPolynomial.taylor_C` turn `taylor α (C c * P)` into `C c * taylor α P`. `← MvPolynomial.smul_eq_C_mul` rewrites that as `c • taylor α P`, and `MvPolynomial.support_smul_eq hc` gives equal supports.
- **Hypotheses**: `hc : c ≠ 0`, which is necessary (evidence (1)). No positivity is needed on `r`, and no nonvanishing is needed on `P`.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.index`, `MvPolynomial.taylor`, `MvPolynomial.taylor_C` (Roth/Taylor.lean:55)
- **Used by**: Roth/Theta.lean:405 (`sum_max_sub_le_index_det`, sign factor `Equiv.Perm.sign s` of a Leibniz-expansion term). It is used nowhere else; `grep -rn index_C_mul Roth/` returns only the declaration and that call.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 194-197 (docstring 194, statement 195-196, proof 197), proof length 1 line
- **Notes**: Formalises the end of clean.tex §4: "For nonzero c ... ind(cP;α;r) = ind(P;α;r)" (clean.tex:636-640). The Lean note at clean.tex:627-634 also asks for "invariance under nonzero scalars" as its own lemma. `Roth.index_neg` is its `c = -1` case (see Roth.index_neg.md).

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_C_mul.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_C_mul.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.index_mul_left_weights`

### `theorem Roth.index_mul_left_weights`
- **Type**: `{σ : Type*} [Fintype σ] {l : ℕ} (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) : Roth.index P α (fun j ↦ l * r j) = Roth.index P α r / l`
- **What**: Scaling all index weights `r j` by the same natural number `l` divides the index by `l`.
- **How**: Proves the image identity `weight (l r) '' supp = (l : ℝ)⁻¹ • (weight r '' supp)` with `Set.image_smul`, `Set.image_image`, `Set.image_congr` and the project's `Roth.weight_mul_left`, then unfolds `index` and pulls the scalar out of the infimum with `Real.sInf_smul_of_nonneg` (`(l:ℝ)⁻¹ ≥ 0` by `positivity`).
- **Hypotheses**: none. In particular no `0 < l` and no `P ≠ 0` (with `l = 0` both sides are `0`; evidence (2)).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.index`, `Roth.weight`, `Roth.weight_mul_left` (Roth/Box.lean:110), `MvPolynomial.taylor` (Roth/Taylor.lean)
- **Used by**: Roth/Theta.lean:447 (`rw [index_mul_left_weights] at hUΘ hVΘ`, in the Lemma 6 product bound). No other use (`grep -rn index_mul_left_weights Roth/`).
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 199-207 (docstring 199, statement 200-201, proof 202-207), proof length 6 lines
- **Notes**: Formalises clean.tex:636-640, `ind(P;α;lr) = (1/l) ind(P;α;r)` stated for `l > 0`; the Lean form drops `l > 0`. The Lean note at clean.tex:627-634 asks for "scaling of the index weights" as a separate lemma.

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_mul_left_weights.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_mul_left_weights.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.index_rename`

### `theorem Roth.index_rename`
- **Type**: `{σ : Type*} [Fintype σ] {τ : Type*} [Fintype τ] {f : σ → τ} (hf : Function.Injective f) (P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ) (α : τ → ℝ) (r : τ → ℕ) : Roth.index (rename f P) α r = Roth.index P (α ∘ f) (r ∘ f)`
- **What**: Embedding a real polynomial into a larger set of variables by an injective renaming does not change its Roth index, provided the point and the weights are pulled back along the renaming.
- **How**: Unfolds both indices (`sInf` of `weight r` over the support of the translate). `MvPolynomial.taylor_rename` (project) turns `taylor α (rename f P)` into `rename f (taylor (α ∘ f) P)`. Mathlib's `MvPolynomial.support_rename_of_injective hf` turns the support into `Finset.image (Finsupp.mapDomain f) _`, and `Finset.coe_image` with `Set.image_image` reduce the problem to a pointwise equality of weights. That equality is `Roth.weight_mapDomain hf r d` (project).
- **Hypotheses**: `hf : Function.Injective f`, which is necessary (evidence (1)). `[Fintype τ]` is needed because `index` is defined on `τ`. No positivity is needed on `r`, and no nonvanishing is needed on `P`.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.index`, `Roth.weight` (via the unfolded `index`), `MvPolynomial.taylor`, `MvPolynomial.taylor_rename` (Roth/Taylor.lean:164), `Roth.weight_mapDomain` (Roth/Box.lean:127)
- **Used by**: Roth/Theta.lean:154 (`simp [map_rename, index_rename e.injective, Function.comp_def]`, an equivalence relabelling) and Roth/Theta.lean:442 (used twice, `index_rename (Option.some_injective _)` and `index_rename hinjV`, in the disjoint-variable product step). It is also mentioned in the module docstring at Roth/Index.lean:39. There are no other uses (`grep -rn index_rename Roth/`).
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 208-219 (docstring 208-211, statement 212-214, proof 215-219), proof length 5 lines
- **Notes**: Formalises the "embedded polynomial keeps its index" step in the proof of clean.tex Lemma 4-2 (clean.tex:614-624). The Lean note at clean.tex:627-634 asks for "invariance under nonzero scalars and variable renaming ... as separate lemmas". The pointwise helper `Roth.weight_mapDomain` carries a superfluous `hf` (see Roth.weight_mapDomain.md), but this lemma needs `hf` anyway for `support_rename_of_injective`.

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_rename.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_rename.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.index_le_card`

### `theorem Roth.index_le_card`
- **Type**: `{σ : Type*} [Fintype σ] {P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ} (hP : P ≠ 0) {r : σ → ℕ} (hd : ∀ j, degreeOf j P ≤ r j) (α : σ → ℝ) : Roth.index P α r ≤ Fintype.card σ`
- **What**: If a real polynomial has degree at most `r j` in each variable `x_j`, then its Roth index at any point, with weights `r`, is at most the number of variables.
- **How**: `Roth.exists_weight_eq_index hP α r` gives a multi-index `d` with `(Δ_d P)(α) ≠ 0` whose weight is the index. The private `mem_support_taylor_iff` puts `d` in the support of `taylor α P`. Mathlib's `MvPolynomial.monomial_le_degreeOf` bounds `d j` by `degreeOf j (taylor α P)`. The project's `MvPolynomial.degreeOf_taylor_le` bounds that by `degreeOf j P`, and `hd j` bounds it by `r j`. Finally `Roth.weight_le_card` turns `d ≤ r` into `weight r d ≤ card σ`.
- **Hypotheses**: `hP : P ≠ 0` is superfluous, because `index 0 α r = sInf ∅ = 0` (evidence (1)). `hd` is necessary (evidence (3)). There is no positivity hypothesis on `r`; `r j = 0` forces `d j = 0` and the junk `0/0 = 0` term.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.index`, `Roth.exists_weight_eq_index` (Roth/Index.lean:76), `Roth.mem_support_taylor_iff` (private, Roth/Index.lean:62), `MvPolynomial.taylor`, `MvPolynomial.degreeOf_taylor_le` (Roth/Taylor.lean:170), `Roth.weight_le_card` (Roth/Box.lean:103), `Roth.weight` (via the `hdw ▸`)
- **Used by**: Roth/Theta.lean:91 (`indexSet_bddAbove`) and Roth/Theta.lean:117 (`Θ_le_card`). Both have the same shape, `index_le_card ((map_ne_zero_iff _ (map_injective _ Int.cast_injective)).2 hR0) (by simpa [degreeOf, degrees_map_of_injective ...] using hRd) _`. There are no other uses (`grep -rn index_le_card Roth/`).
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 221-229 (docstring 221-223, statement 224-225, proof 226-229), proof length 4 lines
- **Notes**: Formalises the source's justification of `Θ_m ≤ m` (clean.tex:665-667, "Taylor's theorem gives a nonzero derivative with `0 ≤ i_j ≤ r_j`, of weight at most `m`"). The source sets `ind(0) = +∞` (clean.tex:545), whereas Lean's junk value is `index 0 = 0`. Because of that junk value the Lean statement holds for `P = 0` as well.

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_le_card.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_le_card.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.index_unique`

### `theorem Roth.index_unique`
- **Type**: `{σ : Type*} [Fintype σ] [Unique σ] {P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ} (hP : P ≠ 0) (α : σ → ℝ) (r : σ → ℕ) : index P α r = (Polynomial.rootMultiplicity (α default) (uniqueAlgEquiv ℝ σ P) : ℝ) / r default`
- **What**: In one variable, the index of a nonzero polynomial `P` at `α` with weight `r` equals the multiplicity of `α` as a root of `P`, divided by `r`.
- **How**: Sets `q = Polynomial.taylor (α default) (uniqueAlgEquiv ℝ σ P)`. A `key` step identifies `eval α (hasseDeriv d P)` with `q.coeff (d default)` via `Polynomial.taylor_coeff`, the project `MvPolynomial.uniqueAlgEquiv_hasseDeriv`, `Finsupp.unique_single` and `MvPolynomial.eval₂_uniqueAlgEquiv`. It rewrites with mathlib's `Polynomial.rootMultiplicity_eq_natTrailingDegree` and `Polynomial.taylor_apply`. It then proves `le_antisymm`: `≤` from `index_le_weight` at `single default q.natTrailingDegree` (using `Polynomial.trailingCoeff_nonzero_iff_nonzero`), and `≥` from `le_index_iff` together with `Polynomial.natTrailingDegree_le_of_ne_zero`.
- **Hypotheses**: `hP : P ≠ 0`. It is superfluous: both sides are `0` at `P = 0` (evidence (1)). No positivity of `r` is assumed.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.index`, `Roth.index_le_weight`, `Roth.le_index_iff`, `Roth.weight` (via `simpa [weight]`), `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv`, `MvPolynomial.uniqueAlgEquiv_hasseDeriv` (Roth/HasseDeriv.lean:266).
- **Used by**: Roth/Theta.lean:242 (`index_unique_le_log_div`, private, which feeds `Θ_unique_le` = source Lemma 5). The reports for `index_le_card` and `index_sub_weight_le_index_hasseDeriv` also use it in their evidence counterexamples.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 231–251, proof length 15 lines
- **Notes**: Listed in the module docstring's main results ("the index ... in one variable"). The source is clean.tex §5, proof of Lemma 5: "Taylor's theorem gives ind(R; h_1/q_1; r_1) = t/r_1".

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_unique.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.index_unique.md)_

### `Roth/Separation.lean`

#### `theorem Roth.eq_mapDomain_some_add_single`

### `theorem Roth.eq_mapDomain_some_add_single`
- **Type**: `{τ : Type*} (d : Option τ →₀ ℕ) : d = (Finsupp.some d).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none (d none)`
- **What**: Every multi-index on the variable set `Option τ` splits as its restriction to `τ`, pushed back along `some`, plus its `none`-component placed at `none`.
- **How**: `ext o`, then `cases o` and `simp` with `Finsupp.mapDomain_of_notMem_range` (the `none` case), `Finsupp.mapDomain_apply_of_injective (Option.some_injective τ)` and `Finsupp.some_apply` (the `some` case).
- **Hypotheses**: none beyond the type `τ` (the section's `[Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ]` are omitted).
- **Uses from project**: `[]`
- **Used by**: Roth/Separation.lean:107 (`rw [coeff_coeffNone, ← eq_mapDomain_some_add_single]` inside `eq_sum_X_none_pow_mul_coeffNone`). No other uses anywhere in `Roth/`.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 55–61, proof length 3 lines
- **Notes**: Holds for any `AddCommMonoid M` in place of `ℕ` (evidence (4)). Mathlib's canonical spelling of the same decomposition is `Finsupp.optionElim_some : f.some.optionElim (f none) = f`.

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.eq_mapDomain_some_add_single.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.eq_mapDomain_some_add_single.md)_

#### `lemma Roth.coeff_X_none_pow_mul_rename`

### `theorem Roth.coeff_X_none_pow_mul_rename`
- **Type**: `∀ {τ R : Type*} [CommSemiring R] (ν : ℕ) (Q : MvPolynomial τ R) (e : Option τ →₀ ℕ), (X none ^ ν * rename some Q).coeff e = if e none = ν then Q.coeff (Finsupp.some e) else 0`
- **What**: In polynomials over the variables `Option τ` (with `none` the distinguished variable), the coefficient of the monomial `x^e` in `x_none^ν · Q(x_τ)` is the coefficient of `Q` at the restriction of `e` to `τ` when the `none`-exponent of `e` equals `ν`, and zero otherwise. Separation-of-variables coefficient formula for a pure power of the distinguished variable times a polynomial in the other variables.
- **How**: Shows `(optionEquivLeft R τ).toAlgHom.comp (rename some) = Polynomial.CAlgHom` by `ext i; simp`, hence `optionEquivLeft R τ (rename some Q) = Polynomial.C Q` (`AlgHom.congr_fun`). Then rewrites the coefficient through `optionEquivLeft_coeff_some_coeff_none` (backwards), `map_mul`, `map_pow`, `optionEquivLeft_X_none`, `Polynomial.X_pow_mul_C`, `Polynomial.C_mul_X_pow_eq_monomial`, `Polynomial.coeff_monomial`, and finishes by `by_cases he : e none = ν` with `simp`.
- **Hypotheses**: `[CommSemiring R]` only (the section's `[Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ]` are omitted); `classical` inside the proof.
- **Uses from project**: `[]` (only mathlib lemmas)
- **Used by**: Roth/Separation.lean: `eq_sum_X_none_pow_mul_coeffNone` (l.104, `simp_rw [coeff_X_none_pow_mul_rename]`) only; `grep -rn coeff_X_none_pow_mul_rename Roth/` gives the definition (l.64) and that single use. Transitively everything downstream of `eq_sum_X_none_pow_mul_coeffNone` (the separated representation in Lemma 2, `exists_separated_representation`, `exists_det_eq_mul`).
- **Visibility**: private
- **Lines**: 63–77, proof length 12 lines
- **Notes**: Declared `private lemma` (kind recorded as theorem). The auxiliary fact `optionEquivLeft R τ (rename some Q) = Polynomial.C Q` is proved verbatim again inside `coeff_rename_mul_aeval` (l.124–127); mathlib has no lemma for it (it is derivable from `optionEquivLeft_symm_apply` + `Polynomial.aevalTower_C`).

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.coeff_X_none_pow_mul_rename.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.coeff_X_none_pow_mul_rename.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.coeff_coeffNone`

### `theorem Roth.coeff_coeffNone`
- **Type**: `{τ : Type*} {R : Type*} [CommSemiring R] (P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) (ν : ℕ) (d : τ →₀ ℕ) : (coeffNone P ν).coeff d = P.coeff (d.mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none ν)` (section `[Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ]` omitted via `omit ... in`; confirmed by `#check`, evidence (0))
- **What**: The coefficient of `x_τ^d` in the `x_none^ν`-coefficient of `P` is the coefficient of `x_τ^d x_none^ν` in `P`.
- **How**: `rw [coeffNone, optionEquivLeft_coeff_coeff]`, then `congr 1; ext o; cases o <;> simp [Finsupp.mapDomain_of_notMem_range, Finsupp.mapDomain_apply_of_injective (Option.some_injective τ)]` to identify `d.optionElim ν` with `d.mapDomain some + single none ν`.
- **Hypotheses**: `[CommSemiring R]` only.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.coeffNone`
- **Used by**: `Roth.eq_sum_X_none_pow_mul_coeffNone` (Roth/Separation.lean:107, `rw [coeff_coeffNone, ← eq_mapDomain_some_add_single]`), `Roth.coeff_rename_mul_aeval` (Roth/Separation.lean:129, `rw [← coeff_coeffNone, coeffNone, ...]`). No uses outside Separation.lean (`grep -rn coeff_coeffNone Roth/`).
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 84–91, proof length 5 lines
- **Notes**: The only content beyond mathlib is the Finsupp identity `d.mapDomain some + single none ν = d.optionElim ν`, which mathlib does not state but which is not needed if the canonical `optionElim` spelling is used (evidence (2)).

#### `theorem Roth.eq_sum_X_none_pow_mul_coeffNone`

### `theorem Roth.eq_sum_X_none_pow_mul_coeffNone`
- **Type**: `{τ : Type*} {R : Type*} [CommSemiring R] (P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) {n : ℕ} (hn : degreeOf none P ≤ n) : P = ∑ ν ∈ Finset.range (n + 1), X none ^ ν * rename some (coeffNone P ν)`. The section's `[Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ]` are omitted via `omit ... in` (confirmed by `#check`, evidence (0)).
- **What**: If `P` has degree at most `n` in `x_none`, then `P = ∑_{ν ≤ n} x_none^ν · φ_ν(x_τ)`, where `φ_ν = coeffNone P ν`.
- **How**: Coefficientwise (`ext e`, `coeff_sum`). The private `coeff_X_none_pow_mul_rename` turns each summand's coefficient into an `if`, and `Finset.sum_ite_eq` collapses the sum. In range, it uses `coeff_coeffNone` plus `eq_mapDomain_some_add_single`. Out of range, it uses `monomial_le_degreeOf` with `hn` to show the coefficient is zero.
- **Hypotheses**: `[CommSemiring R]`; `hn : degreeOf none P ≤ n`.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.coeffNone`, `Roth.coeff_coeffNone`, `Roth.eq_mapDomain_some_add_single`, private `Roth.coeff_X_none_pow_mul_rename`.
- **Used by**: `Roth.exists_separated_representation` (Roth/Separation.lean:335, `exact eq_sum_X_none_pow_mul_coeffNone P hn`) only (`grep -rn` over `Roth/`).
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 93–108, proof length 9 lines (100–108)
- **Notes**: It is the only user of the private `coeff_X_none_pow_mul_rename` and of `eq_mapDomain_some_add_single`. Both become dead once the proof is replaced by the mathlib composition.

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.eq_sum_X_none_pow_mul_coeffNone.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.eq_sum_X_none_pow_mul_coeffNone.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.degreeOf_coeffNone_le`

### `theorem Roth.degreeOf_coeffNone_le`
- **Type**: `∀ {τ R : Type*} [CommSemiring R] (P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) (ν : ℕ) (j : τ), degreeOf j (coeffNone P ν) ≤ degreeOf (some j) P` (section `[Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ]` omitted via `omit ... in`)
- **What**: Taking the coefficient of `x_none^ν` (as a polynomial in the remaining variables `τ`) does not increase the degree in any remaining variable `x_j`.
- **How**: `degreeOf_le_iff` reduces to monomials `d` in the support of `coeffNone P ν`; `mem_support_coeff_optionEquivLeft` turns `d` into `d.optionElim ν ∈ P.support`; `monomial_le_degreeOf (some j)` plus `simpa` (`Finsupp.optionElim` at `some j` is `d j`) closes it. Relies on `coeffNone` unfolding definitionally to `(optionEquivLeft R τ P).coeff ν`.
- **Hypotheses**: none beyond `[CommSemiring R]`.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.coeffNone`
- **Used by**: unused in project (grep of `Roth/` and `Roth.lean` finds only the declaration at Roth/Separation.lean:114)
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 111–116, proof length 2 lines
- **Notes**: Source-cited (§2, Lemma 2, "the ψ_ν's of degree at most r_j in x_j") but the formal proof of `exists_separated_representation` never needs it. It is exactly mathlib's spelling after unfolding `coeffNone` (evidence (2),(3)).

#### `theorem Roth.coeff_rename_mul_aeval`

### `theorem Roth.coeff_rename_mul_aeval`
- **Type**: `{τ : Type*} {R : Type*} [CommSemiring R] (U : MvPolynomial τ R) (V : Polynomial R) (a : τ →₀ ℕ) (b : ℕ) : (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none) V).coeff (a.mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none b) = U.coeff a * V.coeff b` (section `[Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ]` omitted; confirmed by `#check`, evidence (0))
- **What**: In `R[x_τ, x_*]`, the coefficient of `x_τ^a x_*^b` in the separated product `U(x_τ) V(x_*)` is `U_a · V_b`.
- **How**: Transports to `R[x_τ][X]` via `optionEquivLeft`: `rw [← coeff_coeffNone, coeffNone, map_mul]`, identifies `optionEquivLeft (rename some U) = Polynomial.C U` through the local `AlgHom` equation `h` (proved by `ext i; simp`), then `Polynomial.aeval_algHom_apply`, `optionEquivLeft_X_none`, `Polynomial.aeval_X_left_eq_map`, `Polynomial.coeff_C_mul`, `Polynomial.coeff_map`, `algebraMap_eq`, `coeff_C_mul`, `mul_comm`.
- **Hypotheses**: `[CommSemiring R]` only.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.coeff_coeffNone`, `Roth.coeffNone`
- **Used by**: `Roth.index_det_le` in Roth/Theta.lean:480 and :486 (`rw [hF, coeff_rename_mul_aeval, Int.cast_mul, abs_mul] at h1`, bounding coefficients of `U` and `V`); private `exists_int_polynomial_of_primitive_factor_aux` in Roth/Separation.lean:476 (`simp only [coeff_rename_mul_aeval, coeff_map] at h`).
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 117–133, proof length 7 lines
- **Notes**: The `AlgHom` equation `h : (optionEquivLeft R τ).toAlgHom.comp (rename some) = Polynomial.CAlgHom` is re-proved identically in the private `coeff_X_none_pow_mul_rename` (Separation.lean:67); it is the Option analogue of mathlib's `sumAlgEquiv_comp_rename_inr`, which mathlib lacks.

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.coeff_rename_mul_aeval.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.coeff_rename_mul_aeval.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.aeval_X_eq_rename`

### `theorem Roth.aeval_X_eq_rename`
- **Type**: `{R : Type*} [CommSemiring R] {σ : Type*} (i : σ) (f : Polynomial R) : Polynomial.aeval (X i : MvPolynomial σ R) f = rename (fun _ : Unit ↦ i) ((uniqueAlgEquiv R Unit).symm f)` (confirmed by `#check`, evidence (0); the section's `[Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ]` do not appear since `τ` is unused)
- **What**: Substituting `X ↦ X_i` in a univariate polynomial `f` equals viewing `f` as a one-variable `MvPolynomial Unit` and renaming that variable to `i`.
- **How**: term proof `AlgHom.congr_fun (Polynomial.toMvPolynomial_eq_rename_comp i) f`, using that `Polynomial.toMvPolynomial i` is definitionally `aeval (X i)`.
- **Hypotheses**: `[CommSemiring R]` only.
- **Uses from project**: `[]`
- **Used by**: `Roth.hasseDeriv_rename_mul_aeval` (Roth/Separation.lean:162, `simp [aeval_X_eq_rename, degreeOf, ...]`), the degree lemma at Roth/Separation.lean:207 (`simp [aeval_X_eq_rename, ...]`), `Roth.degreeOf_none_rename_mul_aeval` (Roth/Separation.lean:224, `rw [..., aeval_X_eq_rename]`), Roth/Theta.lean:437 (`rw [aeval_X_eq_rename, _root_.map_mul, map_rename, ...]`), Roth/Theta.lean:528 (`rw [aeval_X_eq_rename, map_eq_zero_iff _ (rename_injective _ ...)]`).
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 134–140, proof length 1 line
- **Notes**: Mathlib states the same identity at the `AlgHom` level for `Polynomial.toMvPolynomial i`; the project consistently writes `Polynomial.aeval (X i)` instead, so the mathlib lemma does not fire by `rw`/`simp` without first unfolding `toMvPolynomial` (evidence (3)).

#### `theorem Roth.hasseDeriv_rename_mul_aeval`

### `theorem Roth.hasseDeriv_rename_mul_aeval`
- **Type**: `{τ R : Type*} [CommSemiring R] (d : τ →₀ ℕ) (ν : ℕ) (U : MvPolynomial τ R) (V : Polynomial R) : hasseDeriv (d.mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none ν) (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none) V) = rename some (hasseDeriv d U) * Polynomial.aeval (X none) (Polynomial.hasseDeriv ν V)` (section `[Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ]` omitted; confirmed by `#check`, evidence (0))
- **What**: The Hasse derivative `Δ_{d + ν e_none}` of a separated product `U(x_τ) V(x_none)` is `(Δ_d U)(x_τ) · (D^{(ν)} V)(x_none)`.
- **How**: Splits `Δ_{d+νe_none} = Δ_d ∘ Δ_{νe_none}` with `hasseDeriv_hasseDeriv_of_disjoint` (supports disjoint since `none ∉ range some`), pulls `rename some U` out of `Δ_{νe_none}` with `hasseDeriv_mul_of_degreeOf_eq_zero` (degree of `rename some U` in `none` is 0, via `degrees_rename_of_injective`), applies `hasseDeriv_single_aeval_X`, pulls `aeval (X none) (D^ν V)` out of `Δ_{d.mapDomain some}` by the same lemma (via `aeval_X_eq_rename`, `Multiset.mem_replicate`), and finishes with `hasseDeriv_rename (Option.some_injective τ)`.
- **Hypotheses**: `[CommSemiring R]` only.
- **Uses from project**: `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv`, `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv_hasseDeriv_of_disjoint`, `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv_mul_of_degreeOf_eq_zero`, `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv_single_aeval_X`, `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv_rename`, `Roth.aeval_X_eq_rename`
- **Used by**: `Roth.det_hasseDeriv_separated` in Roth/Separation.lean:365 (`rw [mul_comm (...), hasseDeriv_rename_mul_aeval]`, entries of `A Bᵀ`). No other use in `Roth/`.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 141–166, proof length 19 lines
- **Notes**: Same disjoint-variables / non-canonical-index pattern as `Roth.coeff_rename_mul_aeval` (index `d.mapDomain some + single none ν` instead of mathlib's `d.optionElim ν`).

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.hasseDeriv_rename_mul_aeval.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.hasseDeriv_rename_mul_aeval.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm`

### `theorem Roth.coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm`
- **Type**: `∀ {R : Type*} [CommSemiring R] {σ : Type*} [Unique σ] (f : Polynomial R) (n : ℕ), ((uniqueAlgEquiv R σ).symm f).coeff (Finsupp.single default n) = f.coeff n`
- **What**: Viewing a univariate polynomial `f` as a polynomial in one variable indexed by a singleton type `σ`, the coefficient of the monomial `x^n` is the `n`-th coefficient of `f`.
- **How**: `rw [MvPolynomial.coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm, Finsupp.single_eq_same]` -- mathlib's lemma gives `f.coeff ((single default n) default)`, which evaluates to `f.coeff n`.
- **Hypotheses**: `R` a commutative semiring, `σ` with `[Unique σ]`; no others.
- **Uses from project**: `[]`
- **Used by**: unused in project (`grep -rn coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm Roth/` finds only the declaration itself and three uses that are explicitly qualified `MvPolynomial.coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm`: Roth/Separation.lean:179, Roth/Separation.lean:182, Roth/Theta.lean:429)
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 169-171, proof length 1 line
- **Notes**: Same short name as mathlib's `MvPolynomial.coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm` (Mathlib/Algebra/MvPolynomial/Equiv.lean:115). Inside `namespace Roth` that invites ambiguity, which is presumably why all callers spell out the `MvPolynomial.` prefix. The docstring cites Source §5, proof of Lemma 6, but the only use for that lemma (Theta.lean:429) calls the mathlib lemma directly.

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.degreeOf_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm`

### `theorem Roth.degreeOf_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm`
- **Type**: `∀ {R : Type*} [CommSemiring R] {σ : Type*} [Unique σ] (f : Polynomial R), degreeOf default ((uniqueAlgEquiv R σ).symm f) = f.natDegree`
- **What**: If you view a univariate polynomial `f` as a polynomial in the single variable indexed by a singleton type `σ`, its degree in that variable equals `f.natDegree`.
- **How**: `le_antisymm`. For `≤`, `degreeOf_le_iff` reduces to support monomials `e`. Then `MvPolynomial.coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm` turns `e ∈ support` into `f.coeff (e default) ≠ 0`, and `Polynomial.le_natDegree_of_ne_zero` closes it. For `≥`, `Polynomial.natDegree_le_iff_coeff_eq_zero` reduces to `n` above the degreeOf, and `MvPolynomial.notMem_support_of_degreeOf_lt` applied at `Finsupp.single default n`, combined with `coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm` and `Finsupp.single_eq_same`, closes it.
- **Hypotheses**: `[CommSemiring R]` and `[Unique σ]` only.
- **Uses from project**: `[]` (the `coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm` calls are explicitly qualified `MvPolynomial.`, so they refer to mathlib's lemma, not `Roth.coeff_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm`)
- **Used by**: `Roth.degreeOf_none_rename_mul_aeval` (Roth/Separation.lean:226, `.trans (degreeOf_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm V)`); `Roth.index_separated_mul_le_aux` (private, Roth/Theta.lean:427, `rw [..., degreeOf_uniqueAlgEquiv_symm]`). Both are bare-name uses that resolve to `Roth.` (no mathlib decl of that name exists).
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 173-185, proof length 10 lines
- **Notes**: Docstring has no source citation. Lives in `section General` (`[CommSemiring R]`) of Roth/Separation.lean.

#### `theorem Roth.degreeOf_rename_mul_aeval`

### `theorem Roth.degreeOf_rename_mul_aeval`
- **Type**: `∀ {τ : Type*} {R : Type*} [CommRing R] [IsDomain R] (U : MvPolynomial τ R) {V : Polynomial R}, V ≠ 0 → ∀ (j : τ), degreeOf (some j) (rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none) V) = degreeOf j U` (section `[Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ]` omitted; confirmed by `#check` in the evidence file)
- **What**: In `R[x_τ, x_*]` over a domain, for `V ≠ 0` the degree of the separated product `U(x_τ) V(x_*)` in a variable `x_j`, `j ∈ τ`, equals the degree of `U` in `x_j`.
- **How**: Case `U = 0` by `simp`. Otherwise `rw [degreeOf_mul_eq ...]` (nonzero factors from `rename_eq_zero_iff_of_injective` and `map_ne_zero_iff` with `Polynomial.toMvPolynomial_injective`), then `degreeOf_rename_of_injective (Option.some_injective τ)`; the remaining `degreeOf (some j) (aeval (X none) V) = 0` is closed by `simp [aeval_X_eq_rename, degreeOf, degrees_rename_of_injective ..., Multiset.mem_replicate]`.
- **Hypotheses**: `[CommRing R] [IsDomain R]` (section variables), `hV : V ≠ 0`.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.aeval_X_eq_rename`
- **Used by**: `Roth.exists_det_eq_mul` in Roth/Separation.lean:554 (`rw [← degreeOf_rename_mul_aeval U hV j, ← hFUV]`, bounding `degreeOf j U` by the degree of the determinant `F`). No other uses in `Roth/`.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 193–207, proof length 9 lines
- **Notes**: Twin of `Roth.degreeOf_none_rename_mul_aeval` (line 213), which handles the `none` variable. The `CommRing + IsDomain` section variables are stronger than needed: `CommSemiring + NoZeroDivisors` suffices (evidence (4), (5)).

#### `lemma Roth.linearIndependent_coordinate_polynomials_aux`

### `theorem Roth.linearIndependent_coordinate_polynomials_aux`
- **Type**: `∀ {R M : Type*} [CommRing R] [AddCommMonoid M] [Module R M] {n l : ℕ} (v : Fin (n + 1) → M) (b : Module.Basis (Fin l) R (Submodule.span R (Set.range v))), LinearIndependent R (fun ρ ↦ ∑ ν : Fin (n + 1), Polynomial.C (b.repr ⟨v ν, Submodule.subset_span ⟨ν, rfl⟩⟩ ρ) * Polynomial.X ^ (ν : ℕ))` (declared `private lemma`; the section variables `τ`, `Fintype τ`, `DecidableEq τ` are omitted and `K` is not used).
- **What**: Let `v_0, …, v_n` be vectors in an `R`-module and `b_1, …, b_l` a basis of their span, with `v_ν = ∑_ρ a_{νρ} b_ρ`. Then the `l` polynomials `φ_ρ(X) = ∑_ν a_{νρ} X^ν ∈ R[X]` are linearly independent over `R`. Equivalently, the columns of the `(n+1) × l` coordinate matrix `(a_{νρ})` are linearly independent, because the `v_ν` span. This is the "the `φ_ν` are linearly independent" claim in the separated decomposition of §2, proof of Lemma 2.
- **How**: `Fintype.linearIndependent_iff`; given `∑ ρ g ρ • φ ρ = 0`, take the `ν`-th coefficient (`Polynomial.finsetSum_coeff`, `Polynomial.coeff_C_mul_X_pow`, `Finset.sum_eq_single`) to get `∑_ρ g ρ a_{νρ} = 0` for every `ν`. Then the functional `f = ∑_ρ g ρ • b.coord ρ` on the span vanishes on every `v ν`, hence is zero by `LinearMap.ext_on` with `Submodule.span_span_coe_preimage`. Evaluating `f` at `b ρ₀` (`Module.Basis.coord_apply`, `Module.Basis.repr_self`, `Finsupp.single_apply`, `Finset.sum_ite_eq`) gives `g ρ₀ = 0`.
- **Hypotheses**: `[CommRing R]` (ring, not just semiring, because `Fintype.linearIndependent_iff` is used; commutativity is needed for `g ρ • b.coord ρ` to be a linear map). `[AddCommMonoid M] [Module R M]`. No field assumption: it is stated in more generality than the caller needs (the caller uses a field `K`). No redundant hypotheses.
- **Uses from project**: `[]` (only mathlib: `Fintype.linearIndependent_iff`, `Polynomial.finsetSum_coeff`, `Polynomial.coeff_C_mul_X_pow`, `Finset.sum_eq_single`, `Module.Basis.coord`, `LinearMap.ext_on`, `Submodule.span_span_coe_preimage`, `Module.Basis.repr_self`, `LinearMap.congr_fun`).
- **Used by**: Roth/Separation.lean:339 `Roth.exists_separated_representation` only (supplies `LinearIndependent K φ`); `grep -rn linearIndependent_coordinate_polynomials Roth/` returns only the definition (l.235) and this use. Transitively everything downstream of the separated representation (Lemma 2).
- **Visibility**: private
- **Lines**: 234–262 (including the `omit` line), proof length 22 lines
- **Notes**: Name ends in `_aux`, no docstring (usual for private helpers). The `⟨v ν, Submodule.subset_span ⟨ν, rfl⟩⟩` subtype term is repeated verbatim in the caller and in `eq_sum_aeval_basis_coeffNone_aux`, so the three statements match syntactically.

#### `lemma Roth.eq_sum_aeval_basis_coeffNone_aux`

### `theorem Roth.eq_sum_aeval_basis_coeffNone_aux`
- **Type**: `∀ {τ : Type*} {R : Type*} [CommSemiring R] {P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R} {n l : ℕ}, (P = ∑ ν : Fin (n + 1), X none ^ (ν : ℕ) * rename some (coeffNone P ν)) → ∀ (b : Module.Basis (Fin l) R (Submodule.span R (Set.range (fun ν : Fin (n + 1) ↦ coeffNone P ν)))), P = ∑ ρ, Polynomial.aeval (X none : MvPolynomial (Option τ) R) (∑ ν : Fin (n + 1), Polynomial.C (b.repr ⟨coeffNone P ν, Submodule.subset_span ⟨ν, rfl⟩⟩ ρ) * Polynomial.X ^ (ν : ℕ)) * rename some (b ρ : MvPolynomial τ R)` (declared `private lemma`; section variables `[Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ]` omitted, the section's field `K` unused, its own `R` introduced locally).
- **What**: Suppose `P = ∑_{ν ≤ n} x_none^ν · c_ν(x_τ)` where `c_ν = coeffNone P ν`, and let `b_1, …, b_l` be a basis of the `R`-span of `c_0, …, c_n`, with `c_ν = ∑_ρ a_{νρ} b_ρ`. Then `P = ∑_ρ φ_ρ(x_none) · b_ρ(x_τ)` where `φ_ρ(X) = ∑_ν a_{νρ} X^ν`. This is the identity part of the separated decomposition in §2, proof of Lemma 2 (swap the order of summation after expanding each coefficient polynomial in the basis).
- **How**: Names `a ν ρ` for the coordinates; proves `hexp : coeffNone P ν = ∑ ρ, a ν ρ • b ρ` from `Module.Basis.sum_repr` pushed through `Subtype.val` (`Submodule.coe_sum`, `Submodule.coe_smul`). Rewrites the LHS with `hP`, then `simp_rw [hexp, map_sum, Finset.mul_sum]`, `Finset.sum_comm`, `Finset.sum_mul`, and termwise `map_smul`, `smul_eq_C_mul`, `map_mul`, `Polynomial.aeval_C`, `algebraMap_eq`, `map_pow`, `Polynomial.aeval_X`, closing with `ac_rfl`.
- **Hypotheses**: `[CommSemiring R]` only (more general than the caller's field `K`); `hP` the expansion of `P` in powers of `x_none` up to degree `n` (supplied by the caller from `eq_sum_X_none_pow_mul_coeffNone`); `b` any finite basis of the span. No redundant hypotheses: `hP` is essential (it encodes `degreeOf none P ≤ n`).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.coeffNone` (Roth/Separation.lean:80). Everything else is mathlib.
- **Used by**: Roth/Separation.lean:342 `Roth.exists_separated_representation` only (supplies the final identity `P = ∑ ν, aeval (X none) (φ ν) * rename some (ψ ν)`); `grep -rn eq_sum_aeval_basis_coeffNone_aux Roth/` returns only the definition (l.265) and this use.
- **Visibility**: private
- **Lines**: 264–288 (including the `omit` line), proof length 16 lines
- **Notes**: Name ends in `_aux`, no docstring (usual for private helpers). The subtype term `⟨coeffNone P ν, Submodule.subset_span ⟨ν, rfl⟩⟩` and the `φ_ρ` expression match the caller and `linearIndependent_coordinate_polynomials_aux` syntactically, so the three pieces plug together in one `refine`. The `change` step merely unfolds the local `a`.

#### `lemma Roth.exists_coeffNone_basis_aux`

### `theorem Roth.exists_coeffNone_basis_aux`
- **Type**: `∀ {τ : Type*} {K : Type*} [Field K] {P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K}, P ≠ 0 → ∀ {n : ℕ}, (P = ∑ ν : Fin (n + 1), X none ^ (ν : ℕ) * rename some (coeffNone P ν)) → ∃ l, ∃ b : Module.Basis (Fin l) K (Submodule.span K (Set.range (fun ν : Fin (n + 1) ↦ coeffNone P ν))), 1 ≤ l ∧ l ≤ n + 1 ∧ ∀ ρ j, degreeOf j (b ρ : MvPolynomial τ K) ≤ degreeOf (some j) P` (declared `private lemma`; section variables `[Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ]` omitted).
- **What**: For a nonzero `P = ∑_{ν ≤ n} x_none^ν c_ν(x_τ)` over a field (with `c_ν = coeffNone P ν`), the span `V` of `c_0, …, c_n` has a basis `b_1, …, b_l` with `1 ≤ l ≤ n + 1`, and every basis vector has degree in `x_j` at most `degreeOf (some j) P`. This is the basis-choice step of the separated decomposition in §2, proof of Lemma 2.
- **How**: `V` is finite-dimensional by `FiniteDimensional.span_of_finite`; take `b := Module.finBasis K V`, so `l = finrank K V`. `1 ≤ l`: some `c_ν ≠ 0` (else `hP'` with `simp` forces `P = 0`), then `Module.finrank_pos_iff_exists_ne_zero`. `l ≤ n + 1`: `finrank_range_le_card` and `Fintype.card_fin`. Degree bound: `V ≤ restrictSupport K {d | ∀ j, d j ≤ degreeOf (some j) P}` by `Submodule.span_le` + `Set.range_subset_iff`, each generator handled with `mem_support_coeff_optionEquivLeft` and `monomial_le_degreeOf`; then `degreeOf_le_iff` applied to `(b ρ).2`.
- **Hypotheses**: `[Field K]` (needed for finite-dimensionality/`finBasis` and `finrank_pos`; a free-module version over a PID would also work but is not needed); `hP : P ≠ 0` (essential for `1 ≤ l`); `hP'` the expansion of `P` in powers of `x_none` up to `n` (used only to derive some `c_ν ≠ 0`; it encodes `degreeOf none P ≤ n`). The degree bound and `l ≤ n + 1` do not need `hP'`. No redundant hypotheses for the statement as consumed.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.coeffNone` (Roth/Separation.lean:80, defined as `(optionEquivLeft R τ P).coeff ν`, which is why the mathlib lemma `MvPolynomial.mem_support_coeff_optionEquivLeft` applies after unfolding). All other identifiers (`restrictSupport`, `monomial_le_degreeOf`, `degreeOf_le_iff`, `finrank_range_le_card`, `Module.finBasis`, `Module.finrank_pos_iff_exists_ne_zero`, `FiniteDimensional.span_of_finite`) are mathlib (checked by grep in `.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/`).
- **Used by**: Roth/Separation.lean:336 `Roth.exists_separated_representation` only (`obtain ⟨l, b, hl1, hl2, hdegree⟩ := exists_coeffNone_basis_aux hP hP'`); `grep -rn exists_coeffNone_basis_aux Roth/` returns only the definition (l.291) and this use.
- **Visibility**: private
- **Lines**: 290–316 (including the `omit` line), proof length 21 lines
- **Notes**: Name ends in `_aux`, no docstring (usual for private helpers). Part of a clean three-helper split of `exists_separated_representation` (basis existence here, linear independence in `linearIndependent_coordinate_polynomials_aux`, identity in `eq_sum_aeval_basis_coeffNone_aux`). The basis type is stated on the exact submodule the other two helpers expect, so they plug together in one `refine`.

#### `theorem Roth.exists_separated_representation`

### `theorem Roth.exists_separated_representation`
- **Type**: `{τ : Type*} {K : Type*} [Field K] {P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K} (hP : P ≠ 0) {n : ℕ} (hn : degreeOf none P ≤ n) : ∃ l, 1 ≤ l ∧ l ≤ n + 1 ∧ ∃ (φ : Fin l → Polynomial K) (ψ : Fin l → MvPolynomial τ K), LinearIndependent K φ ∧ LinearIndependent K ψ ∧ (∀ ν, (φ ν).natDegree ≤ n) ∧ (∀ ν j, degreeOf j (ψ ν) ≤ degreeOf (some j) P) ∧ P = ∑ ν, Polynomial.aeval (X none) (φ ν) * rename some (ψ ν)` (`[Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ]` are omitted via `omit ... in`).
- **What**: Over a field, a nonzero polynomial `P(x_τ, x_none)` of degree at most `n` in `x_none` can be written as `∑_{ν<l} φ_ν(x_none) ψ_ν(x_τ)`. Here `1 ≤ l ≤ n+1`, both families are linearly independent, `deg φ_ν ≤ n`, and `deg_{x_j} ψ_ν ≤ deg_{x_j} P`.
- **How**: Expand `P = ∑_{ν ≤ n} x_none^ν · coeffNone P ν` (`eq_sum_X_none_pow_mul_coeffNone`). Take a basis `b` of the span of the coefficient polynomials (`exists_coeffNone_basis_aux`, via `Module.finBasis`, `finrank_range_le_card`, `restrictSupport`). Set `ψ_ρ = b ρ`, which is independent by `Module.Basis.linearIndependent.map'` with `Submodule.ker_subtype`. Set `φ_ρ = ∑_ν (b.repr (coeffNone P ν) ρ) X^ν`, which is independent by `linearIndependent_coordinate_polynomials_aux`. The identity comes from `eq_sum_aeval_basis_coeffNone_aux`, and the degree bound from `Polynomial.natDegree_sum_le_of_forall_le` and `Polynomial.natDegree_C_mul_X_pow_le`.
- **Hypotheses**: `[Field K]` (basis of a subspace), `hP : P ≠ 0` (only for `1 ≤ l`), `hn` (degree bound in `x_none`).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.coeffNone`, `Roth.eq_sum_X_none_pow_mul_coeffNone`, private `Roth.exists_coeffNone_basis_aux`, private `Roth.linearIndependent_coordinate_polynomials_aux`, private `Roth.eq_sum_aeval_basis_coeffNone_aux`.
- **Used by**: `Roth.exists_rat_factors_det_hasseDeriv_aux` (private, Roth/Separation.lean:521–523). It uses `l, hl1, hl2, φ, ψ, hφ, hψ, hPQ` and discards both degree-bound conclusions (`-, -`). Also mentioned in the module docstring (line 31). No other uses in `Roth/`.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 318–345 (statement 325–330), proof 15 lines (331–345)
- **Notes**: The source (clean.tex §2, proof of Lemma 2) gets independence from a minimal-`l` argument. The Lean proof follows the source's Lean implementation note instead (clean.tex:428, "basis of the span of the coefficient polynomials").

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.exists_separated_representation.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.exists_separated_representation.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.det_hasseDeriv_separated`

### `theorem Roth.det_hasseDeriv_separated`
- **Type**: `{τ K : Type*} [Field K] {l : ℕ} (D : Fin l → τ →₀ ℕ) (φ : Fin l → Polynomial K) (ψ : Fin l → MvPolynomial τ K) : (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦ hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none ν) (∑ ρ, Polynomial.aeval (X none) (φ ρ) * rename some (ψ ρ))).det = rename some (genWronskian D ψ) * Polynomial.aeval (X none) (wronskian φ)` (section `[Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ]` omitted; confirmed by `#check`, evidence (0))
- **What**: For a separated sum `P = ∑_ρ φ_ρ(x_none) ψ_ρ(x_τ)`, the determinant `det(Δ_{(D μ, ν)} P)_{μ,ν}` equals `GW_D(ψ)(x_τ) · W(φ)(x_none)`, the product of the generalized Wronskian of `ψ` and the (Hasse) Wronskian of `φ`.
- **How**: Unfolds `genWronskian`, `wronskian`; pushes `rename some` and `aeval (X none)` inside the determinants with `AlgHom.map_det`; rewrites `det B = det Bᵀ` (`Matrix.det_transpose`) and combines with `Matrix.det_mul`; then entrywise (`Matrix.ext`, `map_sum`, `Matrix.mul_apply`, `Finset.sum_congr`) each summand is handled by `hasseDeriv_rename_mul_aeval` after `mul_comm`, closing with `rfl`.
- **Hypotheses**: `[Field K]` (section variable; only needed because the parent defs `genWronskian`, `wronskian` are stated over a field).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.genWronskian`, `Roth.wronskian`, `Roth.wronskianMatrix`, `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv`, `Roth.hasseDeriv_rename_mul_aeval`
- **Used by**: `Roth.exists_rat_factors_det_hasseDeriv_aux` (private) at Roth/Separation.lean:529 (`rw [hFdef, RingHom.map_det, ← det_hasseDeriv_separated D φ ψ, ← hPQ]`). Also named in the module docstring (Separation.lean:33). No other use in `Roth/`.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 351–366, proof length 11 lines
- **Notes**: Matrix index `(D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none ν` is the non-canonical spelling flagged in the `Roth.hasseDeriv_rename_mul_aeval` report (mathlib normal form `(D μ).optionElim ν`).

#### `theorem Roth.exists_sum_mul_eq_gcd`

### `theorem Roth.exists_sum_mul_eq_gcd`
- **Type**: `∀ {ι : Type u_1} (s : Finset ι) (u : ι → ℤ), ∃ z : ι → ℤ, ∑ i ∈ s, z i * u i = s.gcd u` (confirmed by `#check`, evidence (0))
- **What**: Bézout's identity for a finite family of integers: the (normalized) finset gcd of `u` over `s` is an integer linear combination of the `u i`, `i ∈ s`.
- **How**: one line, `simpa [eq_comm, mul_comm] using Finset.gcd_eq_sum_mul s u` (mathlib, Mathlib/RingTheory/PrincipalIdealDomain.lean:233), only flipping the equation and the factor order.
- **Hypotheses**: none beyond the data `s`, `u`; ring fixed to `ℤ`.
- **Uses from project**: `[]`
- **Used by**: `Roth.exists_int_eq_of_gcd_eq_one` (Roth/Separation.lean:446, `obtain ⟨z, hz⟩ := exists_sum_mul_eq_gcd s u`). No other uses (`grep -rn exists_sum_mul_eq_gcd Roth/`).
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 372–376, proof length 1 line
- **Notes**: Section `Integrality` of Separation.lean; docstring cites the source's Lean implementation note ("by a Bézout relation", clean.tex:432).

#### `theorem Roth.exists_nat_smul_eq_map`

### `theorem Roth.exists_nat_smul_eq_map`
- **Type**: `∀ {σ : Type u_1} (G : MvPolynomial σ ℚ), ∃ N U, 0 < N ∧ (map (Int.castRingHom ℚ)) U = ↑N • G` (from `#check`, evidence (0)), with `N : ℕ` and `U : MvPolynomial σ ℤ`
- **What**: clearing denominators. Every rational multivariate polynomial `G` has a positive integer multiple `N • G` whose coefficients all lie in ℤ, i.e. `N • G` is the image of an integer polynomial `U`.
- **How**: takes `N := ∏ d ∈ G.support, (G.coeff d).den`. For each `d` it shows `N * G.coeff d` is an integer using `Finset.dvd_prod_of_mem` and `Rat.den_mul_eq_num` (and `notMem_support_iff` off the support). It then `choose`s those integers `c d`, sets `U := ∑ d ∈ G.support, monomial d (c d)`, and checks coefficients using `coeff_map`, `coeff_sum`, `coeff_smul`, `coeff_monomial` and `Finset.sum_ite_eq'`. Positivity comes from `Finset.prod_pos` and `Rat.den_pos`.
- **Hypotheses**: none. Base rings are fixed to ℤ ⊂ ℚ, and the multiplier must be a positive natural number.
- **Uses from project**: `[]`
- **Used by**: `Roth.exists_primitive_smul_eq` (Roth/Separation.lean:410, `obtain ⟨N, U0, hN, hU0⟩ := exists_nat_smul_eq_map G`). There are no other uses (`grep -rn exists_nat_smul_eq_map Roth/`).

- **Notes**: Section `Integrality` of Separation.lean. The docstring cites clean.tex §2, proof of Lemma 2 ("by clearing denominators"). The only consumer needs `N ≠ 0` as a rational, not `N : ℕ` with `0 < N`.

#### `theorem Roth.exists_primitive_smul_eq`

### `theorem Roth.exists_primitive_smul_eq`
- **Type**: `∀ {σ : Type u_1} {G : MvPolynomial σ ℚ}, G ≠ 0 → ∃ g U, g ≠ 0 ∧ (map (Int.castRingHom ℚ)) U = g • G ∧ (U.support.gcd fun d => U.coeff d) = 1` (from `#check`, evidence (0)), with `g : ℚ`, `U : MvPolynomial σ ℤ`.
- **What**: every nonzero rational multivariate polynomial has a nonzero rational multiple that is an integer polynomial whose coefficients have gcd 1 (a primitive integral polynomial).
- **How**: clears denominators with `Roth.exists_nat_smul_eq_map` to get `U0` with `map U0 = N • G`; shows `U0.support` is nonempty; applies `Finset.extract_gcd` to the coefficient function to get `g'` with `U0.coeff d = g0 * g' d` and `gcd g' = 1`; builds `U := ∑ d ∈ U0.support, monomial d (g' d)` (coefficients via `coeff_monomial`, `Finset.sum_ite_eq'`); takes `g := N / g0`; proves the map identity coefficientwise (`coeff_map`, `coeff_smul`, `eq_div_iff`) and transfers the gcd via `U.support = U0.support` and `Finset.gcd_congr`.
- **Hypotheses**: `hG : G ≠ 0` (essential: for `G = 0` the gcd condition forces `U ≠ 0`, impossible). Base rings fixed to ℤ ⊂ ℚ.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.exists_nat_smul_eq_map`
- **Used by**: `Roth.exists_int_factors` (Roth/Separation.lean:491, `obtain ⟨g, U, hg, hUmap, hgcd⟩ := exists_primitive_smul_eq hU`). No other uses (`grep -rn exists_primitive_smul_eq Roth/`).
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 402–437 (statement 406–408), proof length 29 lines
- **Notes**: Section `Integrality`. Docstring cites clean.tex §2, proof of Lemma 2 ("Choose g ∈ ℚ× so that U = gG is an integral primitive polynomial"). The gcd conclusion is consumed by `exists_int_polynomial_of_primitive_factor_aux` (via `exists_int_eq_of_gcd_eq_one`).

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.exists_primitive_smul_eq.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.exists_primitive_smul_eq.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.exists_int_eq_of_gcd_eq_one`

### `theorem Roth.exists_int_eq_of_gcd_eq_one`
- **Type**: `∀ {ι : Type u_1} {s : Finset ι} {u : ι → ℤ}, s.gcd u = 1 → ∀ {v : ℚ}, (∀ a ∈ s, ∃ z : ℤ, ↑(u a) * v = ↑z) → ∃ z : ℤ, v = ↑z` (from `#check`, evidence (0))
- **What**: if integers `u_a` (`a ∈ s`) have gcd 1 and every product `u_a v` with a rational `v` is an integer, then `v` is an integer.
- **How**: Bézout via `Roth.exists_sum_mul_eq_gcd` (`∑ z_i u_i = 1`), `choose!` integer witnesses `w_i` for `u_i v`, witness `∑ z_i w_i`, and a two-step `calc` using `Finset.sum_mul`, `Finset.sum_congr`, `mul_assoc`, `push_cast`.
- **Hypotheses**: `hu : s.gcd u = 1` (primitivity); `hv : ∀ a ∈ s, ∃ z : ℤ, (u a : ℚ) * v = z`.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.exists_sum_mul_eq_gcd`
- **Used by**: `Roth.exists_int_polynomial_of_primitive_factor_aux` (private, Roth/Separation.lean:473). No other use (`grep -rn exists_int_eq_of_gcd_eq_one Roth/`).
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 440–455 (statement 443–444), proof length 11 lines
- **Notes**: Section `Integrality` of Separation.lean. The docstring cites clean.tex §2, proof of Lemma 2 (lines 420–425: "Primitivity gives a finite integral linear combination ∑ z_a u_a = 1"). The prior report Roth.exists_sum_mul_eq_gcd (REPLACE) already gives a compiled reproof of this theorem via `Finset.gcd_eq_sum_mul`.

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.exists_int_eq_of_gcd_eq_one.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.exists_int_eq_of_gcd_eq_one.md)_

#### `lemma Roth.rescale_separated_factor_aux`

### `theorem Roth.rescale_separated_factor_aux`
- **Type**: `∀ {τ K : Type*} [Field K] {U' U : MvPolynomial τ K} {V' : Polynomial K} {F : MvPolynomial (Option τ) K} {g : K}, g ≠ 0 → U = g • U' → F = rename some U' * Polynomial.aeval (X none) V' → F = rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none) (Polynomial.C g⁻¹ * V')`
- **What**: Rescaling of a separated factorisation. If F = U'(x_τ) V'(x_none) and U = g U' with g a nonzero scalar, then F = U(x_τ) (g⁻¹ V')(x_none): the scalar moved into the first factor is compensated by its inverse in the second.
- **How**: A single `rw` chain: substitute `hF`, `hUmap`; `map_mul` and `Polynomial.aeval_C` + `algebraMap_eq` turn `aeval (X none) (C g⁻¹ * V')` into `C g⁻¹ * aeval (X none) V'`; `smul_eq_C_mul`, `map_mul`, `rename_C` turn `rename some (g • U')` into `C g * rename some U'`; then `mul_mul_mul_comm`, `← C_mul`, `mul_inv_cancel₀ hg`, `C_1`, `one_mul`.
- **Hypotheses**: `[Field K]` (only `g⁻¹` with `mul_inv_cancel₀` and commutativity are needed, so a `Semifield` would suffice); `hg : g ≠ 0`; `hUmap : U = g • U'`; `hF` the given factorisation. Section variables `[Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ]` are omitted (`omit ... in`).
- **Uses from project**: `[]` (only mathlib lemmas: `map_mul`, `Polynomial.aeval_C`, `MvPolynomial.algebraMap_eq`, `MvPolynomial.smul_eq_C_mul`, `MvPolynomial.rename_C`, `mul_mul_mul_comm`, `MvPolynomial.C_mul`, `mul_inv_cancel₀`, `MvPolynomial.C_1`, `one_mul`)
- **Used by**: Roth/Separation.lean: `exists_int_factors` (l.484; `have hF' := rescale_separated_factor_aux hg hUmap hF` at l.492, with `K := ℚ`, `U := map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) U`). `grep -rn rescale_separated_factor_aux Roth/` gives only the definition (l.457) and that use. Transitively: `exists_det_eq_mul` (Lemma 2, l.540) and downstream.
- **Visibility**: private
- **Lines**: 457–463 (with `omit` line 456), proof length 2 lines
- **Notes**: Declared `private lemma` (kind recorded as theorem). The output `hF'` is consumed twice in `exists_int_factors`: by `exists_int_polynomial_of_primitive_factor_aux` (l.493) and in the `map_injective` rewrite (l.502). Generic over a field K although only instantiated at ℚ.

#### `lemma Roth.exists_int_polynomial_of_primitive_factor_aux`

### `theorem Roth.exists_int_polynomial_of_primitive_factor_aux`
- **Type**: `∀ {τ : Type u_1} {U : MvPolynomial τ ℤ} {V' : Polynomial ℚ} {F : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ}, U.support.gcd ⇑U.coeff = 1 → map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) F = rename some (map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) U) * Polynomial.aeval (X none) V' → ∃ V : Polynomial ℤ, Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) V = V'`
- **What**: Integrality of the second factor in a separated factorisation. If U is an integral polynomial in the variables x_τ whose coefficients have gcd 1, and the integral polynomial F in x_τ, x_none equals U(x_τ) V'(x_none) over ℚ, then V' has integral coefficients. (Coefficient of x^a x_none^b in F is u_a v_b; primitivity of U gives an integral combination of the u_a equal to 1.)
- **How**: Reduces via `Polynomial.mem_map_range (Int.castRingHom ℚ)` to showing each coefficient `V'.coeff b` lies in the range of the cast; for each b applies the project lemma `exists_int_eq_of_gcd_eq_one hgcd` with witness `F.coeff (a.mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none b)`, obtained by taking that coefficient of `hF` and simplifying with the project lemma `coeff_rename_mul_aeval` and `MvPolynomial.coeff_map`.
- **Hypotheses**: `hgcd : U.support.gcd U.coeff = 1` (primitivity of U); `hF` the rational factorisation of the integral F. Section variables `[Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ]` are omitted (`omit ... in`). No nonvanishing hypotheses are needed (all used).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.exists_int_eq_of_gcd_eq_one` (Roth/Separation.lean:443), `Roth.coeff_rename_mul_aeval` (Roth/Separation.lean:123). Mathlib: `Polynomial.mem_map_range`, `MvPolynomial.coeff_map`.
- **Used by**: Roth/Separation.lean: `exists_int_factors` (l.493, `obtain ⟨V, hVmap⟩ := exists_int_polynomial_of_primitive_factor_aux hgcd hF'`). `grep -rn exists_int_polynomial_of_primitive_factor_aux Roth/` gives only the definition (l.466) and that use. Transitively: `exists_det_eq_mul` (Lemma 2) and downstream.
- **Visibility**: private
- **Lines**: 466–478 (with `omit` line 465), proof length 9 lines
- **Notes**: Declared `private lemma` (kind recorded as theorem). Consumes the output `hF'` of `rescale_separated_factor_aux`. This is the mathematical heart of the "U, V integral" step of the source's proof of Lemma 2 (§2), the local Gauss-lemma argument specialised to separated variables. No docstring (private helper).

#### `theorem Roth.exists_int_factors`

### `theorem Roth.exists_int_factors`
- **Type**: `∀ {τ : Type u_1} {U' : MvPolynomial τ ℚ} {V' : Polynomial ℚ}, U' ≠ 0 → V' ≠ 0 → ∀ {F : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ}, map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) F = rename some U' * Polynomial.aeval (X none) V' → ∃ U V, U ≠ 0 ∧ V ≠ 0 ∧ F = rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none) V ∧ (∀ j, degreeOf j U = degreeOf j U') ∧ V.natDegree = V'.natDegree` (from `#check`, evidence (0)).
- **What**: if an integer polynomial `F(x_τ, y)` factors over ℚ as `U'(x_τ) V'(y)` with nonzero factors, then it factors over ℤ as `U(x_τ) V(y)` with nonzero factors of the same partial degrees.
- **How**: `exists_primitive_smul_eq` gives `g ≠ 0` and primitive integral `U` with `map U = g • U'`; `rescale_separated_factor_aux` rewrites the factorisation as `rename U * aeval (C g⁻¹ * V')`; `exists_int_polynomial_of_primitive_factor_aux` (via `exists_int_eq_of_gcd_eq_one` and `coeff_rename_mul_aeval`) shows `C g⁻¹ * V'` has integer coefficients; equality over ℤ by `MvPolynomial.map_injective` + `map_rename` + `Polynomial.map_aeval_eq_aeval_map`; degree equalities via `degrees_map_of_injective`, `degreeOf_C_mul`, `Polynomial.natDegree_map_eq_of_injective`, `Polynomial.natDegree_C_mul`.
- **Hypotheses**: `hU : U' ≠ 0` (needed for the primitive multiple), `hV : V' ≠ 0` (only for the conclusion `V ≠ 0`), `hF` the rational factorisation.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.exists_primitive_smul_eq`, `Roth.rescale_separated_factor_aux` (private), `Roth.exists_int_polynomial_of_primitive_factor_aux` (private; itself uses `Roth.exists_int_eq_of_gcd_eq_one`, `Roth.coeff_rename_mul_aeval`)
- **Used by**: `Roth.exists_det_eq_mul` (Roth/Separation.lean:552, `obtain ⟨U, V, hU, hV, hFUV, -, -⟩ := exists_int_factors hU' hV' hFmap`); mentioned in module docstring Separation.lean:35. No other uses (`grep -rn exists_int_factors Roth/`). Indirectly feeds `Roth/Theta.lean:571` through `exists_det_eq_mul`.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 481–505 (statement 485–489), proof length 16 lines
- **Notes**: The degree-equality conjuncts are discarded by the only caller (`-, -`); degrees are re-derived there from `degreeOf_rename_mul_aeval`. Docstring cites clean.tex §2, proof of Lemma 2.

#### `lemma Roth.exists_rat_factors_det_hasseDeriv_aux`

### `theorem Roth.exists_rat_factors_det_hasseDeriv_aux`
- **Type**: `∀ {τ : Type*} [Finite τ] {P : MvPolynomial (Option τ) ℤ}, P ≠ 0 → ∀ {n : ℕ}, degreeOf none P ≤ n → ∃ l, 1 ≤ l ∧ l ≤ n + 1 ∧ ∃ D : Fin l → (τ →₀ ℕ), (∀ μ, (D μ).degree ≤ μ) ∧ ∃ (U : MvPolynomial τ ℚ) (V : Polynomial ℚ), U ≠ 0 ∧ V ≠ 0 ∧ map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) (Matrix.of fun μ ν : Fin l ↦ hasseDeriv ((D μ).mapDomain some + Finsupp.single none ν) P).det = rename some U * Polynomial.aeval (X none) V`
- **What**: Rational form of Roth's Lemma 2. For a nonzero integer polynomial P in the variables x_τ and x_none with degree at most n in x_none, there are 1 ≤ l ≤ n + 1 and differential operators Δ_μ (multi-indices D μ in the x_τ variables of order at most μ) such that the l×l determinant det(Δ_μ ∂_{x_none}^ν P / ν!) (Hasse derivatives), viewed over ℚ, factors as U(x_τ) V(x_none) with U, V nonzero.
- **How**: Map P to ℚ (PQ); transfer the degree bound via `degrees_map_of_injective`; obtain a separated representation PQ = Σ_ν φ_ν(x_none) ψ_ν(x_τ) with both families linearly independent from `exists_separated_representation` (nonvanishing of PQ via `MvPolynomial.map_injective` + `Int.cast_injective`); choose D with generalized Wronskian of ψ nonzero by `exists_genWronskian_ne_zero` (Lemma 1); rewrite the mapped determinant via `RingHom.map_det`, `hasseDeriv_map` and `det_hasseDeriv_separated` into `rename some (genWronskian D ψ) * aeval (X none) (wronskian φ)`; nonvanishing of V by `wronskian_ne_zero_of_linearIndependent`.
- **Hypotheses**: `[Finite τ]` (needed by `exists_genWronskian_ne_zero`); `hP : P ≠ 0`; `hn : degreeOf none P ≤ n`. Section variables `[Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ]` omitted (`omit ... in`). All hypotheses used.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.hasseDeriv` (Roth/HasseDeriv.lean:57), `Roth.hasseDeriv_map` (Roth/HasseDeriv.lean:134), `Roth.exists_separated_representation` (Roth/Separation.lean:325), `Roth.det_hasseDeriv_separated` (Roth/Separation.lean:351), `Roth.exists_genWronskian_ne_zero` (Roth/GenWronskian.lean:254), `Roth.genWronskian` (Roth/GenWronskian.lean:32), `Roth.wronskian` (Roth/Wronskian.lean:60), `Roth.wronskian_ne_zero_of_linearIndependent` (Roth/Wronskian.lean:205)
- **Used by**: Roth/Separation.lean: `exists_det_eq_mul` (Lemma 2, l.542; call at l.551 `exists_rat_factors_det_hasseDeriv_aux hP (hr none)`). `grep -rn exists_rat_factors_det_hasseDeriv_aux Roth/` gives only the definition (l.510) and this use. Transitively everything downstream of Lemma 2.

- **Lines**: 510–534 (with `omit` line 509), proof length 19 lines
- **Notes**: Declared `private lemma` (kind recorded as theorem). The existential conclusion is the full Lemma 2 shape minus integrality and degree bounds; the witnesses are U = genWronskian D ψ and V = wronskian φ, but these are hidden behind the existential, which is fine because exists_det_eq_mul only needs nonvanishing plus the factorisation. No docstring (private helper).
