#### `lemma Roth.theta_scaled_unique_le_aux`

### `theorem Roth.theta_scaled_unique_le_aux`
- **Type**: `∀ {δ M : ℝ} {q₁ q₂ r₁ r₂ l : ℕ}, 1 < q₁ → 0 < r₁ → 0 < l → (r₁ : ℝ) * Real.log q₁ ≤ r₂ * Real.log q₂ → 1 ≤ M → M ≤ (q₁ : ℝ) ^ (δ * (l * r₁)) → Θ M (fun _ : Unit ↦ q₂) (fun _ ↦ l * r₂) ≤ δ` (declared `private lemma`).
- **What**: In one variable (index type `Unit`), if `q₁ > 1`, `r₁, l > 0`, the balanced-logarithm inequality `r₁ log q₁ ≤ r₂ log q₂` holds and `1 ≤ M ≤ q₁^{δ l r₁}`, then `Θ_1(M; q₂; l r₂) ≤ δ`. This is exactly the displayed chain in the source proof of Lemma 7: `Θ_1(M; q_p; l r_p) ≤ Θ_1(q_1^{δ_1 l r_1}; q_p; l r_p) ≤ log(q_1^{δ_1 l r_1})/(l r_p log q_p) ≤ δ_1` (clean.tex, around l.985), with `δ` playing the role of `δ_1`.
- **How**: First derives side facts: `0 ≤ δ` from `1 ≤ M ≤ q₁^{δ l r₁}` via `Real.rpow_le_rpow_left_iff` and `nonneg_of_mul_nonneg_left`; `0 < r₂ log q₂` from `hqr` and `Real.log_pos`; hence `0 < log q₂` (`pos_of_mul_pos_right`), `0 < r₂` (`pos_of_mul_pos_left`) and `1 < q₂` (`Real.log_pos_iff`). Then chains `Roth.Θ_mono` (to raise `M` to `q₁^{δ l r₁}`) with `Roth.Θ_unique_le` (Lemma 5, `Θ_1(B; q; r) ≤ log B / (r log q)`), rewrites with `Real.log_rpow` and `div_le_iff₀`, and closes with `nlinarith only [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hqr ...]`.
- **Hypotheses**: All six are used in the proof. `hq₁` gives `log q₁ > 0` (needed for `δ ≥ 0` and `q₂ > 1`); `hr₁`, `hl` are needed because if `r₁ = 0` or `l = 0` then `hM` forces `M ≤ 1` with `δ` unconstrained (possibly negative), while `Θ` is nonnegative-valued in that regime, so the conclusion would fail; `hM1` is required by `Θ_mono`/`Θ_unique_le` and also rules out `M < 1` with negative `δ`; `hqr` is the balanced-log comparison that converts the `q₂, r₂` denominator to `q₁, r₁`. (Necessity argued informally from the proof; no weakening experiment run, as the lemma is private.)
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.Θ` (Roth/Theta.lean:68), `Roth.Θ_mono` (Roth/Theta.lean:134), `Roth.Θ_unique_le` (Roth/Theta.lean:256).
- **Used by**: Roth/Theta.lean `Roth.theta_phi_lt_aux` only (l.883, the one-variable term of `Φ`, applied with `δ := ε = δ(1 + 1/(n+2))` via `hM.le`). `grep -rn theta_scaled_unique_le_aux Roth/` returns only the definition (l.742) and this use. Transitively: `theta_phi_lt_aux` → `theta_lt_succ_aux` (l.930) → public `Roth.Θ_lt` (Lemma 7, l.978).
- **Visibility**: private
- **Lines**: 742–765, proof length 21 lines (745–765)
- **Notes**: No docstring (unlike the neighbouring `lem6M_lt`, `lem7_Φ_bound`, `lem7_numeric`, which cite the source). Name ends in `_aux`, so the Step 9a pre-filter would also fire.

#### `lemma Roth.theta_delta_succ_lt_aux`

### `theorem Roth.theta_delta_succ_lt_aux`
- **Type**: `∀ (n : ℕ) {δ : ℝ}, δ ≤ 1 / ((n : ℝ) + 1 + 1) → δ * (1 + 1 / ((n : ℝ) + 1 + 1)) < 1 / ((n : ℝ) + 1)` (declared `private lemma`).
- **What**: With `p = n + 2`, if `δ ≤ 1/p` then `δ_1 := δ(1 + 1/p) < 1/(p-1)`. Mathematically: `δ(1 + 1/p) ≤ (p+1)/p² < 1/(p-1)` because `(p-1)(p+1) = p² - 1 < p²`. This is the source inequality `0 < (p-1)δ_1 < (p-1)(p+1)/p² < 1` (clean.tex l.995, proof of Lemma 7), which certifies that the enlarged parameter `δ_1` satisfies the induction hypothesis' bound `δ_1 ≤ 1/(p-1)`.
- **How**: Records `0 < n+1` and `0 < n+2` by `positivity`; bounds the left side by `(1/(n+2)) * (1 + 1/(n+2))` via `mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hδ (by positivity)`; rewrites the target with `lt_div_iff₀`; closes with `nlinarith only [one_div_mul_cancel (ne_of_gt hN2), sq_pos_of_pos (one_div_pos.mpr hN2)]`.
- **Hypotheses**: The single hypothesis `hδ` is necessary (for large `δ` the conclusion fails). No sign condition on `δ` is needed: the factor `1 + 1/(n+2)` is positive, so negative `δ` is covered by the same monotonicity step. `n` is explicit because it cannot be inferred from the conclusion's use site otherwise (it is passed as `theta_delta_succ_lt_aux n hδ`).
- **Uses from project**: `[]` (only mathlib lemmas: `mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right`, `lt_div_iff₀`, `one_div_mul_cancel`, `sq_pos_of_pos`, `one_div_pos`, tactics `positivity`, `nlinarith`).
- **Used by**: Roth/Theta.lean `Roth.theta_phi_lt_aux` only (l.889, as `(theta_delta_succ_lt_aux n hδ).le`, the `δ`-bound argument to the induction hypothesis `ih` at `ε = δ(1 + 1/(n+2))`). `grep -rn theta_delta_succ_lt_aux Roth/` returns only the definition (l.767) and this use. Transitively: `theta_phi_lt_aux` (l.840) → `theta_lt_succ_aux` (l.891) → public `Roth.Θ_lt` (Lemma 7, l.962).
- **Visibility**: private
- **Lines**: 767–774, proof length 6 lines (769–774)
- **Notes**: No docstring. Name ends in `_aux`, so the Step 9a pre-filter would also fire. A natural generalisation (real `N > 0`, `δ ≤ 1/(N+1)` implies `δ(1 + 1/(N+1)) < 1/N`) exists but has no use.

#### `lemma Roth.theta_prefix_lt_aux`

### `theorem Roth.theta_prefix_lt_aux`
- **Type**: `∀ {n : ℕ} {δ ε M : ℝ}, 0 < δ → δ ≤ ε → ∀ {r q : Fin (n + 2) → ℕ}, (∀ j, r (Fin.last (n + 1)) ≤ r j) → 3 ≤ (r (Fin.last (n + 1)) : ℝ) → (∀ i : Fin (n + 1), (r i.succ : ℝ) ≤ δ * r i.castSucc) → ((n : ℝ) + 1 + 1) * (2 * ((n : ℝ) + 1 + 1) + 1) / δ ≤ Real.log (q 0) → (∀ j, (r 0 : ℝ) * Real.log (q 0) ≤ r j * Real.log (q j)) → ∀ {l : ℕ}, 1 ≤ l → 1 ≤ M → M ≤ (q 0 : ℝ) ^ (ε * (l * r 0)) → (IH for dimension n+1 at parameter ε: for all r q : Fin (n + 1) → ℕ satisfying the four Lemma 7 hypotheses with ε, Θ ((q 0) ^ (ε * r 0)) q r < 10 ^ (n + 1) * ε ^ ((1/2) ^ (n + 1))) → Θ M (fun j : Fin (n + 1) ↦ q j.castSucc) (fun j ↦ l * r j.castSucc) < 10 ^ (n + 1) * ε ^ ((1 / 2 : ℝ) ^ (n + 1))` (declared `private lemma`).
- **What**: In the induction step of Lemma 7 (dimension `p = n + 2`), bounds the `(p-1)`-variable term of `Φ` in Lemma 6: if `1 ≤ M ≤ q_1^{ε l r_1}` (with `ε = δ_1 = δ(1+1/p)` at the call site), then `Θ_{p-1}(M; q_1, …, q_{p-1}; l r_1, …, l r_{p-1}) < 10^{p-1} ε^{(1/2)^{p-1}}`. This is the source passage clean.tex l.989–1007: monotonicity of `Θ` in `M`, then verifying the four induction hypotheses for dimension `p-1`, parameter `δ_1`, degrees `l r_j`, then applying the induction hypothesis.
- **How**: `Θ_mono hM1 hM` reduces to `M = q_1^{ε l r_1}`; a `simp [Nat.cast_mul]` rewrite `hCeq` puts the bound in the shape `(q' 0)^(ε * r' 0)` for the truncated tuples `q' = q ∘ castSucc`, `r' = l * r ∘ castSucc`; then `refine ih …` with four obligations: (1) final-degree bound `3 ≤ l r_{p-1}` from `hrlast`, `hanti` and `le_mul_of_one_le_left`; (2) degree ratios `l r_{i+1} ≤ ε l r_i` by a `calc` with `mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hratio _)`, `mul_left_comm`, `mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hδε`; (3) the log bound `(n+1)(2(n+1)+1)/ε ≤ log q_1` via `div_le_div₀` (numerator decreases from `p(2p+1)`, denominator increases `δ ≤ ε`) composed with `hq1`; (4) balanced logs scaled by `l` via `mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hqr _)` and `simpa only [mul_assoc, Fin.castSucc_zero]`.
- **Hypotheses**: `hδ0` is needed for `div_le_div₀` (positivity of the smaller denominator). `hδε` drives obligations (2) and (3). `hanti` is used only at `j = (Fin.last n).castSucc`, so it could be weakened to that single inequality (cosmetic; the call site passes `fun j ↦ hanti (Fin.le_last j)`). `hl : 1 ≤ l` is needed for (1). `hM1`, `hM` feed `Θ_mono`. `ih` is the induction hypothesis already specialised to parameter `ε`. All hypotheses are used.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.Θ` (def, Roth/Theta.lean:68), `Roth.Θ_mono` (Roth/Theta.lean:134). All other names are mathlib (`div_le_div₀`, `le_mul_of_one_le_left`, `mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left`, `mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right`, `mul_left_comm`, `mul_nonneg`, `add_nonneg`, `Nat.cast_nonneg`, `Nat.cast_mul`, `Fin.castSucc_zero`, `Fin.last`, `Fin.castSucc`; tactics `push_cast`, `linarith`, `norm_num`, `simp`, `simpa`, `exact_mod_cast`).
- **Used by**: Roth/Theta.lean `Roth.theta_phi_lt_aux` only (l.886, inside the final `exact`, as the `(p-1)`-variable summand of `Φ`, with `ε := δ(1 + 1/(n+2))` and `ih hε0 (theta_delta_succ_lt_aux n hδ).le`). `grep -rn theta_prefix_lt_aux Roth/` returns only the definition (l.776) and this use. Transitively: `theta_phi_lt_aux` (l.840) → `theta_lt_succ_aux` (l.891, use at l.930) → public `Roth.Θ_lt` (Lemma 7, l.962).
- **Visibility**: private
- **Lines**: 776–816, proof length 27 lines (790–816; statement 776–789)
- **Notes**: No docstring. Name ends in `_aux`, so the Step 9a pre-filter would also fire. Faithful to the source step (clean.tex l.989–1007); the source's strict inequality `log q_1 > δ_1^{-1}(p-1)(2p-1)` is used here in non-strict form, which is all the IH needs.

#### `lemma Roth.theta_lt_base_aux`

### `theorem Roth.theta_lt_base_aux`
- **Type**: `∀ {δ : ℝ}, 0 < δ → δ ≤ 1 → ∀ {r q : Fin 1 → ℕ}, 0 < r 0 → 1 < q 0 → Θ ((q 0 : ℝ) ^ (δ * r 0)) q r < 10 ^ 1 * δ ^ ((1 / 2 : ℝ) ^ 1)` (declared `private lemma`).
- **What**: The `m = 1` base case of Lemma 7: for one variable with `r_1 > 0`, `q_1 > 1` and `0 < δ ≤ 1`, `Θ_1(q_1^{δ r_1}; q_1; r_1) < 10 δ^{1/2}`. Mirrors clean.tex l.964–967: by Lemma 5, `Θ_1 ≤ δ r_1 log q_1 / (r_1 log q_1) = δ < 10 δ^{1/2}`. The conclusion is stated in the literal shape `10 ^ 1 * δ ^ ((1/2) ^ 1)` of `Θ_lt` at `n = 0`, so it plugs in without rewriting.
- **How**: Step `h`: `Θ_unique_le` (Lemma 5) with `B = q_0^{δ r_0}`, `1 ≤ B` from `Real.one_le_rpow`, gives `Θ ≤ log B / (r_0 log q_0)`; then `Subsingleton.elim (default : Fin 1) 0`, `Real.log_rpow` and `field_simp` (nonvanishing of `r_0` and of `log q_0` via `Real.log_pos`) reduce the right side to `δ`. Step `h1`: `δ ≤ δ^{1/2}` from `Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_ge hδ0 hδ` (needs `δ ≤ 1`). Finally `norm_num only [pow_one]` and `lt_mul_of_one_lt_left (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hδ0 _)` with `1 < 10`.
- **Hypotheses**: `hδ0` for `Real.rpow_pos_of_pos`, positivity of the exponent, and `rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_ge`; `hδ : δ ≤ 1` for `δ ≤ δ^{1/2}` (the call site handles `δ > 1` separately via `theta_lt_of_large_delta_aux`); `hrlast : 0 < r 0` for cancelling `r_0` in `field_simp`; `hq1 : 1 < q 0` for `Θ_unique_le`, `1 ≤ B` and `log q_0 ≠ 0`. All used.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.Θ` (def, Roth/Theta.lean:68), `Roth.Θ_unique_le` (Lemma 5, Roth/Theta.lean:256). All other names are mathlib (`Real.one_le_rpow`, `Real.log_rpow`, `Real.log_pos`, `Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_ge`, `Real.rpow_pos_of_pos`, `lt_mul_of_one_lt_left`, `Subsingleton.elim`, `ne_of_gt`; tactics `field_simp`, `norm_num`, `positivity`, `simpa`, `exact_mod_cast`).
- **Used by**: Roth/Theta.lean `Roth.Θ_lt` only (l.973, `zero` case of the induction, branch `δ ≤ 1`, with `hq1` supplied by `theta_denominators_one_lt_aux`). `grep -rn theta_lt_base_aux Roth/` returns only the definition (l.818) and this use.
- **Visibility**: private
- **Lines**: 818–831, proof length 11 lines (821–831; statement 818–820)
- **Notes**: No docstring. Name ends in `_aux`, so the Step 9a pre-filter would also fire. Faithful to the source base case; the `δ ≤ 1` restriction is an artefact of the Lean statement of `Θ_lt` dropping the source's `δ < 1/m` (the large-δ case is handled trivially elsewhere). Cosmetic: `h1` could be `Real.self_le_rpow_of_le_one hδ0.le hδ (by norm_num)` (as used at l.951), avoiding the `simpa`.

#### `lemma Roth.theta_denominators_one_lt_aux`

### `theorem Roth.theta_denominators_one_lt_aux`
- **Type**: `∀ {n : ℕ} {r q : Fin (n + 1) → ℕ}, 0 < r 0 → 0 < Real.log (q 0) → (∀ j, (r 0 : ℝ) * Real.log (q 0) ≤ r j * Real.log (q j)) → ∀ (j : Fin (n + 1)), 1 < q j` (declared `private lemma`).
- **What**: Under the Lemma 7 growth condition `r_1 log q_1 ≤ r_j log q_j` (clean.tex (21)-type hypothesis `hqr` of `Θ_lt`), together with `r_1 > 0` and `log q_1 > 0`, every denominator satisfies `q_j > 1`. Supplies the `1 < q` side conditions needed by Lemma 5 (`Θ_unique_le`) and by `Real.one_le_rpow`.
- **How**: One term: `mul_pos` gives `0 < r 0 * log (q 0)`; `.trans_le (hqr j)` gives `0 < r j * log (q j)`; `pos_of_mul_pos_right` (with `Nat.cast_nonneg (r j)`) gives `0 < log (q j)`; `Real.log_pos_iff (Nat.cast_nonneg (q j))` converts to `1 < (q j : ℝ)`; `exact_mod_cast` lands in `ℕ`.
- **Hypotheses**: `hrlast : 0 < r 0` and `hq1 : 0 < log (q 0)` both feed `mul_pos` and are necessary (with either zero the left side of `hqr` is `0` and nothing is forced); `hqr` used at index `j`. All used, none redundant. (`hrlast` is a misnomer here: it is positivity of `r 0`, not of the last entry.)

#### `lemma Roth.theta_phi_lt_aux`

### `theorem Roth.theta_phi_lt_aux`
- **Type**: `∀ {n : ℕ} {δ : ℝ}, 0 < δ → δ ≤ 1 / ((n : ℝ) + 1 + 1) → δ < 1 → ∀ {r q : Fin (n + 2) → ℕ}, Antitone r → 3 ≤ (r (Fin.last (n + 1)) : ℝ) → (∀ i : Fin (n + 1), (r i.succ : ℝ) ≤ δ * r i.castSucc) → ((n : ℝ) + 1 + 1) * (2 * ((n : ℝ) + 1 + 1) + 1) / δ ≤ Real.log (q 0) → (∀ j, (r 0 : ℝ) * Real.log (q 0) ≤ r j * Real.log (q j)) → (IH: Lemma 7 for dimension n+1, universally quantified over δ, r, q) → ∀ {l : ℕ}, l ∈ Finset.Icc 1 (r (Fin.last (n + 1)) + 1) → let M := lem6M (n + 2) (r 0) ((q 0 : ℝ) ^ (δ * r 0)) l; let Φ := Θ M (fun _ : Unit ↦ q (Fin.last (n + 1))) (fun _ ↦ l * r (Fin.last (n + 1))) + Θ M (fun j : Fin (n + 1) ↦ q j.castSucc) (fun j ↦ l * r j.castSucc); Φ + √Φ + √δ < (10 ^ (n + 2) * δ ^ ((1 / 2 : ℝ) ^ (n + 2))) / 2` (declared `private lemma`).
- **What**: The per-`l` core of the induction step of Lemma 7 (dimension `p = n + 2`): for every admissible `l ∈ [1, r_p + 1]`, with `M` and `Φ` as in Lemma 6, `Φ + Φ^{1/2} + δ^{1/2} < 10^p δ^{(1/2)^p} / 2`. Source: clean.tex l.969–1018 (estimate of `M`, of the one-variable term by `δ_1`, of the `(p-1)`-variable term by the IH at `δ_1 = δ(1+1/p)`, then the numeric absorption `Φ < 3·10^{p-1} δ^{(1/2)^{p-1}}` and the final bound).
- **How**: Derives `0 < r j` from `hrlast` + `hanti`, `1 < q 0` via `theta_denominators_one_lt_aux`; sets `ε = δ(1 + 1/(n+2))`; `one_le_lem6M` gives `1 ≤ M`; `lem6M_lt` (with `l ≤ r 0` obtained from `l ≤ r_p + 1`, `r_p ≤ r_1 ≤ δ r_0 < r_0` via `hratio 0`, `mul_lt_of_lt_one_left`) gives `M < q_1^{ε l r_1}`; `lem7_Φ_bound` gives `ε + 10^{p-1} ε^{(1/2)^{p-1}} < 3·10^{p-1} δ^{(1/2)^{p-1}}`; then `lt_div_iff₀'` and `lem7_numeric` reduce the goal to `Φ ≤ 3·10^{p-1}δ^{…}`, closed by `add_lt_add_of_le_of_lt` of `theta_scaled_unique_le_aux` (one-variable term `≤ ε`) and `theta_prefix_lt_aux` (prefix term `< 10^{p-1} ε^{…}`, fed `ih hε0 (theta_delta_succ_lt_aux n hδ).le`).
- **Hypotheses**: All are used. `hδ1` and `hanti` are redundant given the others: `hδ1` follows from `hδ` (as derived in `theta_lt_succ_aux`, l.903–904) and `hanti` follows from `hratio` + `hδ1` (as derived via `Fin.antitone_iff_succ_le` at l.905–906); the caller passes them already computed, so this is a deliberate split, cosmetic only. `hl` bounds both sides of `l`; `ih` is the full universally quantified Lemma 7 IH.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.Θ` (Roth/Theta.lean:68), `Roth.lem6M` (l.270), `Roth.one_le_lem6M` (l.279), `Roth.lem6M_lt` (l.649), `Roth.lem7_Φ_bound` (l.678), `Roth.lem7_numeric` (l.709), `Roth.theta_scaled_unique_le_aux` (l.742), `Roth.theta_delta_succ_lt_aux` (l.767), `Roth.theta_prefix_lt_aux` (l.776), `Roth.theta_denominators_one_lt_aux` (l.833). Mathlib names: `Finset.mem_Icc`, `Fin.le_last`, `Real.one_le_rpow`, `mul_pos`, `add_pos`, `mul_nonneg`, `Nat.cast_nonneg`, `Nat.succ_le_succ`, `Nat.succ_le_of_lt`, `Nat.zero_lt_succ`, `mul_lt_of_lt_one_left`, `lt_div_iff₀'`, `add_lt_add_of_le_of_lt`, `le_mul_of_one_le_right`, `le_add_of_nonneg_right`, `Nat.add_sub_cancel`, `Nat.cast_add`, `Nat.cast_one`.
- **Used by**: Roth/Theta.lean `Roth.theta_lt_succ_aux` only (l.930, `exact fun l hl ↦ theta_phi_lt_aux … ih hl` after `Finset.sup'_lt_iff`). `grep -rn theta_phi_lt_aux Roth/` returns only the definition (l.840) and this use. Transitively: `theta_lt_succ_aux` (l.891) → public `Roth.Θ_lt` (Lemma 7, l.962).

#### `lemma Roth.theta_lt_succ_aux`

### `theorem Roth.theta_lt_succ_aux`
- **Type**: `∀ {n : ℕ} {δ : ℝ}, 0 < δ → δ ≤ 1 / ((n : ℝ) + 1 + 1) → ∀ {r q : Fin (n + 2) → ℕ}, 3 ≤ (r (Fin.last (n + 1)) : ℝ) → (∀ i : Fin (n + 1), (r i.succ : ℝ) ≤ δ * r i.castSucc) → ((n : ℝ) + 1 + 1) * (2 * ((n : ℝ) + 1 + 1) + 1) / δ ≤ Real.log (q 0) → (∀ j, (r 0 : ℝ) * Real.log (q 0) ≤ r j * Real.log (q j)) → (ih: Lemma 7 in dimension n+1 for every δ with 0 < δ ≤ 1/(n+1), every r q : Fin (n + 1) → ℕ satisfying the analogous hypotheses) → Θ ((q 0 : ℝ) ^ (δ * r 0)) q r < 10 ^ (n + 2) * δ ^ ((1 / 2 : ℝ) ^ (n + 2))` (declared `private lemma`).
- **What**: The induction step of Lemma 7 for `p = n + 2` variables in the case `δ ≤ 1/p`: assuming Lemma 7 in `p - 1` variables, `Θ_p(q_1^{δ r_1}; q; r) < 10^p δ^{(1/2)^p}`. Source: clean.tex l.969–1021 (proof of `roth:lem7`, inductive step: apply Lemma 6, estimate `M` and `Φ` for every allowed `l`).
- **How**: Derives `δ < 1` from `hδ` (`div_lt_one`), `Antitone r` from `hratio` via `Fin.antitone_iff_succ_le` and `mul_le_of_le_one_left`, `1 < q j` via `theta_denominators_one_lt_aux`, and the Lemma 6 ratio hypothesis `r_last ≤ δ r_j` (`hratio'`, via `Fin.succ_last`, `mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left`). Transports `Θ` along `finSuccEquivLast : Fin (n + 2) ≃ Option (Fin (n + 1))` with `Θ_equiv`, applies Lemma 6 `Θ_option_le` (with `r₁ = r 0`, `B = q_0^{δ r_0} ≥ 1` by `Real.one_le_rpow`), unfolds `lem6Φ`, rewrites with `lt_div_iff₀'` and `Finset.sup'_lt_iff`, and closes each `l` by `theta_phi_lt_aux`.
- **Hypotheses**: All used. `hδ0`, `hδ` give `δ < 1` and feed `Θ_option_le` and `theta_phi_lt_aux`; `hrlast`, `hratio` give antitonicity, positivity and the Lemma 6 ratio condition; `hq1`, `hqr` give `1 < q j` and are forwarded; `ih` is forwarded to `theta_phi_lt_aux`. The `ih` hypothesis carries an upper bound `δ ≤ 1/(n+1)` that the caller discards (`fun {δ} hδ0 _ ↦ ih hδ0`), i.e. it is weaker than the full IH available; this is a deliberate narrowing, no redundancy.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.Θ` (Roth/Theta.lean:68), `Roth.Θ_equiv` (l.159), `Roth.lem6Φ` (l.275), `Roth.Θ_option_le` (l.593, Lemma 6), `Roth.theta_denominators_one_lt_aux` (l.833), `Roth.theta_phi_lt_aux` (l.840). Mathlib names: `div_lt_one`, `Fin.antitone_iff_succ_le`, `mul_le_of_le_one_left`, `Fin.zero_le`, `Fin.le_last`, `Fin.succ_last`, `Fin.castSucc_le_castSucc_iff`, `mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left`, `finSuccEquivLast`, `finSuccEquivLast_symm_none`, `finSuccEquivLast_symm_some`, `Nat.zero_lt_of_lt`, `Real.one_le_rpow`, `Fintype.card_fin`, `lt_div_iff₀'`, `Finset.sup'_lt_iff`, `Function.comp_def`.
- **Used by**: Roth/Theta.lean `Roth.Θ_lt` only (l.978, `succ` case of the induction when `δ ≤ 1/(n+2)`). `grep -rn theta_lt_succ_aux Roth/` returns only the definition (l.891) and this use. Transitively feeds every user of the public Lemma 7 `Roth.Θ_lt`.

#### `lemma Roth.theta_lt_of_large_delta_aux`

### `theorem Roth.theta_lt_of_large_delta_aux`
- **Type**: `∀ {n : ℕ} {δ : ℝ}, 1 / ((n : ℝ) + 1) ≤ δ → ∀ {r q : Fin (n + 1) → ℕ}, Θ ((q 0 : ℝ) ^ (δ * r 0)) q r < 10 ^ (n + 1) * δ ^ ((1 / 2 : ℝ) ^ (n + 1))` (declared `private lemma`).
- **What**: For `m = n + 1` variables and `δ ≥ 1/m`, the Lemma 7 conclusion `Θ_m(q_1^{δ r_1}; q; r) < 10^m δ^{(1/2)^m}` holds unconditionally (no hypotheses on `r`, `q`). This is the range `δ ≥ 1/m` excluded by the source's hypothesis (19) `0 < δ < m^{-1}` (clean.tex l.935–960); the project's `Θ_lt` drops that upper bound, and this lemma discharges the extra case.
- **How**: A `calc` chain: `Θ ≤ m` by `Θ_le_card` (with `Fintype.card_fin` via `simpa`); `m < 10^m / m` via `lt_div_iff₀` and `m·m < 2^m·2^m = 4^m ≤ 10^m` (`Nat.lt_two_pow_self`, `mul_self_lt_mul_self`, `mul_pow`, `pow_le_pow_left₀`); rewrite `10^m / m = 10^m · (1/m)` (`mul_one_div`); finally `1/m ≤ (1/m)^{(1/2)^m} ≤ δ^{(1/2)^m}` by `Real.self_le_rpow_of_le_one` (base `1/m ≤ 1` via `div_le_one`, exponent `≤ 1` via `pow_le_one₀`) and `Real.rpow_le_rpow` with `hδ`, under `mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left`.
- **Hypotheses**: Only `hδ`, and it is used (last `calc` step, `Real.rpow_le_rpow`). It is essential in the sense that for small `δ` the right side tends to `0` while `Θ` can be positive; no hypotheses on `r`, `q` are needed (the bound `Θ ≤ card` is unconditional).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.Θ` (Roth/Theta.lean:68), `Roth.Θ_le_card` (l.114). Mathlib names: `Nat.lt_two_pow_self`, `lt_div_iff₀`, `mul_self_lt_mul_self`, `mul_pow`, `pow_le_pow_left₀`, `mul_one_div`, `mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left`, `Real.self_le_rpow_of_le_one`, `div_le_one`, `pow_le_one₀`, `Real.rpow_le_rpow`, `Nat.cast_nonneg`.
- **Used by**: Roth/Theta.lean `Roth.Θ_lt` only, twice: l.975 (`zero` case, `δ > 1`) and l.980 (`succ` case, `δ > 1/(n+2)`). `grep -rn theta_lt_of_large_delta_aux Roth/` returns only the definition (l.932) and these two uses. Transitively feeds every user of public Lemma 7 `Roth.Θ_lt`.
- **Visibility**: private
- **Lines**: 932–953, proof length 19 lines (935–953; statement 932–934)
- **Notes**: No docstring. Name ends in `_aux`. Not in the source paper as such: it is the formalisation's extension of Lemma 7 to all `δ > 0` (the public `Θ_lt` docstring says "No upper bound on `δ` is required"). The bound is very lossy but suffices.

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.theta_lt_of_large_delta_aux.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.theta_lt_of_large_delta_aux.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.Θ_lt`

### `theorem Roth.Θ_lt`
- **Type**: `(n : ℕ) {δ : ℝ} (hδ0 : 0 < δ) {r q : Fin (n + 1) → ℕ} (hrlast : 3 ≤ (r (Fin.last n) : ℝ)) (hratio : ∀ i : Fin n, (r i.succ : ℝ) ≤ δ * r i.castSucc) (hq1 : ((n : ℝ) + 1) * (2 * ((n : ℝ) + 1) + 1) / δ ≤ Real.log (q 0)) (hqr : ∀ j, (r 0 : ℝ) * Real.log (q 0) ≤ r j * Real.log (q j)) : Θ ((q 0 : ℝ) ^ (δ * r 0)) q r < 10 ^ (n + 1) * δ ^ ((1 / 2 : ℝ) ^ (n + 1))`
- **What**: Lemma 7 of the source (clean.tex §5, Lemma `roth:lem7`, l.935–960): for `m = n + 1` variables, rapidly decreasing weights `r` and denominators `q` with `log q_1` large and balanced `r_j log q_j ≥ r_1 log q_1`, the supremum of indices `Θ_m(q_1^{δ r_1}; q; r)` is `< 10^m δ^{(1/2)^m}`.
- **How**: induction on `n` generalising `δ`. Base case `n = 0`: for `δ ≤ 1`, private `theta_lt_base_aux` (via Lemma 5 `Θ_unique_le`) with `1 < q 0` from private `theta_denominators_one_lt_aux`; for `δ > 1`, private `theta_lt_of_large_delta_aux` (trivial bound `Θ_le_card` and `(n+1)^2 < 4^(n+1)`). Step: for `δ ≤ 1/(n+2)`, private `theta_lt_succ_aux` (Lemma 6 `Θ_option_le` transported along `finSuccEquivLast`, closed by `theta_phi_lt_aux` with the induction hypothesis); otherwise again `theta_lt_of_large_delta_aux`. Mathlib: `not_le`, `Nat.cast_add`, `Nat.cast_one`.
- **Hypotheses**: `hδ0` (positivity of δ; with δ = 0 the RHS is 0 and the claim is false since Θ ≥ 0); `hrlast` (needed by Lemma 6 and positivity of `r 0` in the base case); `hratio` (weight decay, fed to Lemma 6 and the induction); `hq1` (large `log q 0`, gives `q_j > 1` and the Lemma 5 bounds); `hqr` (balanced logarithms). All named in the proof body.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.Θ`, private `Roth.theta_lt_base_aux`, private `Roth.theta_denominators_one_lt_aux`, private `Roth.theta_lt_of_large_delta_aux`, private `Roth.theta_lt_succ_aux` (all in Roth/Theta.lean)
- **Used by**: Roth/MainLemma.lean:687 (private `theta_lt_eta_of_sq_log_bound_aux`, itself used at MainLemma.lean:724 on the path to `main_lemma`); named in the module docstring Roth/Theta.lean:39. No other occurrence in `Roth/` or `Roth.lean`.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 956–981 (docstring 956–961, statement 962–966, proof 967–981), proof length 15 lines
- **Notes**: listed under `## Main results` of Roth/Theta.lean. Hypotheses are weaker than the source: no upper bound `δ < 1/m` (large δ handled by `theta_lt_of_large_delta_aux`), `r_m ≥ 3` instead of `r_m > 10/δ`, non-strict `r_{j} ≤ δ r_{j-1}` and `≤` in (21).

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.Θ_lt.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.Θ_lt.md)_

#### `def Roth.mainCoeffBound`

### `def Roth.mainCoeffBound`
- **Type**: `(δ : ℝ) (q₁ r₁ : ℕ) : ℕ`, defined as `⌊(q₁ : ℝ) ^ (δ * r₁)⌋₊`
- **What**: the integer coefficient bound `B_1 = [q_1^{δ r_1}]` of §6 of the source (clean.tex eq. (37), l.1351–1353); the coefficients of the box polynomials in the pigeonhole family `𝒲` range over `[0, B_1]`, and the Hasse derivatives of the auxiliary polynomial are bounded by `B_1^{1+3δ}`.
- **How**: direct term; `Nat.floor` of the real power `Real.rpow (q₁ : ℝ) (δ * r₁)`.

#### `theorem Roth.exists_delta_bound`

### `theorem Roth.exists_delta_bound`
- **Type**: `{n m : ℕ} (hm : 16 * n ^ 2 < m) : ∃ δ₀ : ℝ, 0 < δ₀ ∧ ∀ δ : ℝ, 0 ≤ δ → δ ≤ δ₀ → δ < 1 / m ∧ eta δ m + 2 * (1 + 3 * δ) * n * √(m : ℝ) < m / 2`
- **What**: Lemma `m-δ` of §6 of the source (clean.tex l.1211–1248), fixed-`m` form: for `m > 16 n²` there is `δ₀ > 0` such that every `δ ∈ [0, δ₀]` satisfies (29) `δ < 1/m` and (30) `10^m δ^{(1/2)^m} + 2(1+3δ) n √m < m/2`.
- **How**: from `hm`, `4n < √m` (`Real.lt_sqrt_of_sq_lt`), hence the gap `2n√m < m/2` (`nlinarith` with `Real.sq_sqrt`); the LHS of (30) is `ContinuousAt` 0 (`Real.continuousAt_rpow_const` plus `fun_prop`) and equals `2n√m` at `δ = 0` (`Real.zero_rpow`); `ContinuousAt.eventually_lt` and `Metric.eventually_nhds_iff` give a radius `ε`; take `δ₀ = min (1/m) ε / 2` (`half_lt_self`, `lt_min_iff`).
- **Hypotheses**: `hm : 16 * n ^ 2 < m` (necessary: the conclusion at `δ = 0` is equivalent to it, compiled in evidence).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.eta`
- **Used by**: `Roth.exists_delta` (Roth/Main.lean:231); module docstring MainLemma.lean:40.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 72–94 (docstring 72–76, statement 77–78), proof length 16 lines
- **Notes**: the source's proof gives an explicit `δ`; the Lean proof uses continuity instead (shorter, matches the source's "tends to 0" remark at l.1230).

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.exists_delta_bound.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.exists_delta_bound.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.eta_lt_gam_iff`

### `theorem Roth.eta_lt_gam_iff`
- **Type**: `(δ : ℝ) (n m : ℕ) : eta δ m + 2 * (1 + 3 * δ) * n * √(m : ℝ) < m / 2 ↔ eta δ m < gam δ n m`
- **What**: inequality (30) of the source, `η + 2(1+3δ) n √m < m/2`, is equivalent to (38) `η < γ`, where `γ = (m - λ)/2` and `λ = 4(1+3δ) n √m`.
- **How**: `grind [gam, lam]`: unfold `gam` and `lam`, then linear arithmetic over ℝ.
- **Hypotheses**: none (holds for all real `δ` and natural `n, m`).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.eta`, `Roth.gam`, `Roth.lam`
- **Used by**: `Roth.exists_delta` (Roth/Main.lean:241, `sub_pos.mpr ((eta_lt_gam_iff 0 n m).mp …)`); a theorem at Roth/Main.lean:560 (`sub_pos.2 ((eta_lt_gam_iff _ _ _).1 h30)`).

#### `lemma Roth.sqrt_lt_nat_floor_aux`

### `theorem Roth.sqrt_lt_nat_floor_aux`
- **Type**: `∀ {X : ℝ}, 2 ≤ X → √X < (⌊X⌋₊ : ℝ)` (declared `private lemma`; `⌊X⌋₊` is `Nat.floor`, cast to `ℝ`).
- **What**: For a real `X ≥ 2`, the square root of `X` is strictly smaller than the natural floor of `X`. This is the strict inequality `B₁ = ⌊X⌋ > √X` of the source's §6 (with `X = q₁^{δ r₁}`), isolated as the first component of `Roth.sqrt_lt_floor`.
- **How**: Term-mode proof. First `hfloor : 2 ≤ (⌊X⌋₊ : ℝ)` by `exact_mod_cast Nat.le_floor hX`. Then reduce `√X < ⌊X⌋₊` to `X < ⌊X⌋₊ ^ 2` via `Real.sqrt_lt'` (positivity of the floor from `hfloor` by `positivity`), and close with `nlinarith only [Nat.lt_floor_add_one X, hfloor]` (from `X < f + 1` and `f ≥ 2` one gets `X < f + 1 ≤ f²`).
- **Hypotheses**: `hX : 2 ≤ X`. Needed in some form: for `X ∈ [1, 2)` the floor is `1` while `√X ≥ 1`, so the strict inequality fails (e.g. `X = 1`: `√1 = 1 = ⌊1⌋₊`); for `X < 1` the floor is `0`. The threshold `2` is not sharp (the claim holds iff `⌊X⌋₊ ≥ 2`, i.e. `X ≥ 2`, since for `X ∈ [2,3)` one has `X < 3 < 4 = 2²`), so `2 ≤ X` is exactly the right hypothesis.
- **Uses from project**: `[]` (only mathlib: `Nat.le_floor`, `Nat.lt_floor_add_one`, `Real.sqrt_lt'`; tactics `exact_mod_cast`, `positivity`, `nlinarith`).
- **Used by**: Roth/MainLemma.lean `Roth.sqrt_lt_floor` only (l.116, `⟨sqrt_lt_nat_floor_aux hX, Nat.floor_le (by positivity)⟩`). `grep -rn sqrt_lt_nat_floor_aux Roth/` returns only the definition (l.104) and this use. (Indirectly, through `sqrt_lt_floor`, it feeds Roth/MainLemma.lean l.154 and l.201 and Roth/Main.lean l.494.)
- **Visibility**: private
- **Lines**: 104–106, proof length 2 lines (105–106)
- **Notes**: Name ends in `_aux`, so the Step 9a pre-filter would also fire. Introduced in commit 9bebcd0 ("Clean up and sharpen the square-root floor bound"), which also weakened `sqrt_lt_floor`'s hypothesis from `4 < X` to `2 ≤ X`; the cleanup audit `.mathlib-quality/cleanup/MainLemma-sqrt_lt_nat_floor_aux.md` recorded the split and the elaboration speed-up (about 129 ms to 96 ms).

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.sqrt_lt_nat_floor_aux.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.sqrt_lt_nat_floor_aux.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.sqrt_lt_floor`

### `theorem Roth.sqrt_lt_floor`
- **Type**: `∀ {X : ℝ}, 2 ≤ X → √X < (⌊X⌋₊ : ℝ) ∧ (⌊X⌋₊ : ℝ) ≤ X`
- **What**: For real `X ≥ 2`, the natural floor of `X` lies strictly above `√X` and at or below `X`. This is the source's §6 estimate `B₁ = ⌊X⌋ > X - 1 > √X` with `X = q₁^{δ r₁}`, together with the retained upper bound `B₁ ≤ X`.
- **How**: Anonymous-constructor pairing: the strict half is the private helper `Roth.sqrt_lt_nat_floor_aux hX` (itself `Real.sqrt_lt'` + `Nat.le_floor` + `Nat.lt_floor_add_one` + `nlinarith`), the upper half is mathlib's `Nat.floor_le (by positivity)`.
- **Hypotheses**: `hX : 2 ≤ X`. Sharp: at `X = 1` the strict half fails (`√1 = 1 = ⌊1⌋₊`, compiled in evidence), and for `X ∈ [1,2)` generally `⌊X⌋₊ = 1 ≤ √X`. The upper half alone needs only `0 ≤ X`.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.sqrt_lt_nat_floor_aux`
- **Used by**: `Roth.mul_log_div_two_lt_log_mainCoeffBound` (Roth/MainLemma.lean:154, `.1`); the `key` step of a theorem at Roth/MainLemma.lean:201 (`.1`); Roth/Main.lean:494 (`obtain ⟨hsqB, hBX⟩ := sqrt_lt_floor ...`, both halves).
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 108–116 (docstring 108–114, statement 115), proof length 1 line
- **Notes**: Hypothesis was weakened from `4 < X` to `2 ≤ X` in commit 9bebcd0 (source has `X > 4`, clean.tex l.1383). The strict half was split off as the private `sqrt_lt_nat_floor_aux` (assessed SKIP).

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.sqrt_lt_floor.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.sqrt_lt_floor.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.four_lt_rpow`

### `theorem Roth.four_lt_rpow`
- **Type**: `∀ {δ : ℝ}, 0 ≤ δ → ∀ {q₁ r₁ : ℕ}, 0 < r₁ → 3 ≤ δ ^ 2 * Real.log ↑q₁ → 4 < (↑q₁ : ℝ) ^ (δ * ↑r₁)`
- **What**: If `δ ≥ 0`, `r₁ ≥ 1` and `δ² log q₁ ≥ 3`, then `X = q₁^{δ r₁} > 4`. This is the source's §6 claim "`X > 4`" (clean.tex l.1367), which there follows from (32) and (29).
- **How**: Derives `log q₁ > 0` (`pos_of_mul_pos_right`), hence `q₁ ≥ 2` (`Real.log_pos_iff`); proves `log 2 < 3/4` via `Real.log_lt_iff_lt_exp` and `Real.quadratic_le_exp_of_nonneg`; reduces the goal with `Real.lt_rpow_of_log_lt` to `log 4 < δ r₁ log q₁`; proves `log 4 = 2 log 2 < δ log q₁` by contradiction with `nlinarith` (using `mul_self_le_mul_self`, `Real.log_le_log`, `Real.log_pos`), then multiplies by `r₁ ≥ 1` (`le_mul_of_one_le_right`).
- **Hypotheses**: `hδ0 : 0 ≤ δ` (necessary; δ = -2, q₁ = 3, r₁ = 1 is a counterexample, compiled), `hr₁ : 0 < r₁` (necessary; r₁ = 0 gives `X = 1`, compiled), `hq₁ : 3 ≤ δ² log q₁` (the constant 3 could be lowered to anything above `4 log 2 ≈ 2.77`, not pursued: no caller benefit).
- **Uses from project**: `[]`
- **Used by**: Roth/MainLemma.lean:641 (`Nat.floor_pos.2 <| (by norm_num : (1 : ℝ) ≤ 4).trans (four_lt_rpow hδ0 hr₁ hq).le`); Roth/Main.lean:495 (`sqrt_lt_floor ((by norm_num : (2 : ℝ) ≤ 4).trans (four_lt_rpow hδ0 hr0 h32').le)`).
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 118–141 (docstring 118–121, statement 122–123), proof length 18 lines
- **Notes**: Both callers use only `.le` and need only `1 ≤ X` resp. `2 ≤ X`; the strict `4 <` mirrors the source's "`X > 4`".

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.four_lt_rpow.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.four_lt_rpow.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.mul_log_div_two_lt_log_mainCoeffBound`

### `theorem Roth.mul_log_div_two_lt_log_mainCoeffBound`
- **Type**: `∀ {δ : ℝ} {q₁ r₁ : ℕ}, 2 ≤ (↑q₁ : ℝ) ^ (δ * ↑r₁) → δ * ↑r₁ * Real.log ↑q₁ / 2 < Real.log ↑(mainCoeffBound δ q₁ r₁)`
- **What**: With `X = q₁^{δ r₁}` and `B₁ = ⌊X⌋₊ = mainCoeffBound δ q₁ r₁`, if `X ≥ 2` then `½ δ r₁ log q₁ < log B₁`. This is the source's §6 step "Thus `log B_1 > ½ δ r_1 log q_1`" (clean.tex l.1371).
- **How**: One `simpa` term: `Real.log_lt_log` applied to `√X > 0` (`Real.sqrt_pos`) and the project's `sqrt_lt_floor hX` (`√X < ⌊X⌋₊`), then rewriting `log √X = ½ log X` (`Real.log_sqrt`) and `log X = δ r₁ log q₁` (`Real.log_rpow`, with `0 < q₁` extracted from `1 < X` via `Real.one_lt_rpow_iff`).
- **Hypotheses**: `hX : 2 ≤ q₁^{δ r₁}` (necessary: δ = 0 gives `X = 1` and the conclusion `0 < log 1` fails, compiled; threshold 2 is sharp in the abstract form: `X = 3/2` fails, compiled).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.mainCoeffBound`, `Roth.sqrt_lt_floor`
- **Used by**: Roth/MainLemma.lean:205 (inside `Roth.three_small_factors`, `mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left (mul_log_div_two_lt_log_mainCoeffBound hX) hδ`). No other uses in `Roth/`.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 143–154 (docstring 143–148, statement 149–150), proof length 4 lines
- **Notes**: none

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.mul_log_div_two_lt_log_mainCoeffBound.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.mul_log_div_two_lt_log_mainCoeffBound.md)_

#### `lemma Roth.two_lt_rpow_aux`

### `theorem Roth.two_lt_rpow_aux`
- **Type**: `∀ {δ : ℝ}, 0 ≤ δ → ∀ {q₁ r₁ : ℕ}, 0 < r₁ → Real.log 2 < δ ^ 2 * Real.log ↑q₁ → 2 < (↑q₁ : ℝ) ^ (δ * ↑r₁)`
- **What**: If `δ ≥ 0`, `r₁ ≥ 1` and `log 2 < δ² log q₁`, then `X = q₁^{δ r₁} > 2`. Mathematically: the hypothesis forces `log q₁ > 0`, hence `q₁ ≥ 2` and `log q₁ ≥ log 2`, so `δ log q₁ = √(δ² log q₁ · log q₁) > √(log 2 · log 2) = log 2`, and multiplying by `r₁ ≥ 1` gives `δ r₁ log q₁ > log 2`.
- **How**: `hlog2 : 0 < log 2` (`Real.log_pos`); `hL : 0 < log q₁` (`pos_of_mul_pos_right` with `sq_nonneg δ`); `hq : 2 ≤ q₁` (`Real.log_pos_iff`, cast); `hr : 1 ≤ (r₁ : ℝ)`; `hprod : log 2 < δ log q₁` by contradiction with `nlinarith only` fed `mul_self_le_mul_self`, `mul_lt_mul_of_pos_right h1 hL` and `mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (Real.log_le_log ...)`; finally `Real.lt_rpow_of_log_lt` reduces the goal to `log 2 < δ r₁ log q₁`, closed by `nlinarith only [hprod, le_mul_of_one_le_right ...]`.
- **Hypotheses**: `hδ0 : 0 ≤ δ` (necessary: δ = -1, q₁ = 3, r₁ = 1 satisfies `log 2 < log 3` but `3^{-1} < 2`); `hr₁ : 0 < r₁` (necessary: r₁ = 0 gives `X = 1`); `h1 : log 2 < δ² log q₁` (essentially sharp shape: at δ² log q₁ = log 2 with q₁ = 2, δ = 1, r₁ = 1 one gets X = 2, so strictness is needed for the strict conclusion).
- **Uses from project**: `[]` (only mathlib lemmas)
- **Used by**: Roth/MainLemma.lean:193 in `Roth.three_small_factors` (`have hX := (two_lt_rpow_aux hδ0 hr₁ (hlog2.trans_le h1)).le`) only; `grep -rn two_lt_rpow_aux Roth/` gives the definition (l.156) and this use. Transitively everything downstream of `three_small_factors`.
- **Visibility**: private
- **Lines**: 156–170, proof length 13 lines
- **Notes**: Declared `private lemma` (kind recorded as theorem); no docstring. The caller only uses `.le` (`2 ≤ X`). Same proof skeleton as the public `Roth.four_lt_rpow` (l.122, hypothesis `3 ≤ δ² log q₁`, conclusion `4 < X`), but neither implies the other at the caller: `three_small_factors` has only `1 ≤ δ² log q₁`, too weak for `four_lt_rpow`.

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.two_lt_rpow_aux.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.two_lt_rpow_aux.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.three_small_factors`

### `theorem Roth.three_small_factors`
- **Type**: `∀ {δ : ℝ}, 0 ≤ δ → ∀ {m q₁ r₁ : ℕ}, 0 < r₁ → ∀ {A α : ℝ}, 0 ≤ A → 2 * ↑m + 1 + 2 * ↑m * Real.log (1 + A) + 2 * ↑m * Real.log (1 + |α|) ≤ δ ^ 2 * Real.log ↑q₁ → 2 ^ (m * r₁) < ↑(mainCoeffBound δ q₁ r₁) ^ δ ∧ (1 + A) ^ (m * r₁) < ↑(mainCoeffBound δ q₁ r₁) ^ δ ∧ (1 + |α|) ^ (m * r₁) < ↑(mainCoeffBound δ q₁ r₁) ^ δ`
- **What**: With `B₁ = ⌊q₁^{δ r₁}⌋` (`mainCoeffBound`), the non-strict form of the source's parameter condition (32), `2m + 1 + 2m log(1+A) + 2m log(1+|α|) ≤ δ² log q₁`, implies the three bounds `2^{m r₁} < B₁^δ`, `(1+A)^{m r₁} < B₁^δ`, `(1+|α|)^{m r₁} < B₁^δ` (source §6, eq. `roth:three-small-factors`, clean.tex l.1374).
- **How**: Nonnegativity of the two logs gives `1 ≤ δ² log q₁`; with `log 2 < 1` (`Real.log_lt_sub_one_of_pos`) the private `two_lt_rpow_aux` gives `X = q₁^{δ r₁} ≥ 2`, hence `δ > 0`. A local `key` lemma reduces `t^{m r₁} < B₁^δ` to `2 m log t ≤ δ² log q₁` via `Real.lt_rpow_of_log_lt`, positivity of `B₁` from `sqrt_lt_floor`, `Real.log_pow`, and `nlinarith` fed with `mul_log_div_two_lt_log_mainCoeffBound` (`log B₁ > ½ δ r₁ log q₁`). The three side conditions are closed by `nlinarith only` from `h32`.
- **Hypotheses**: `hδ0 : 0 ≤ δ`, `hr₁ : 0 < r₁`, `hA : 0 ≤ A`, `h32` (non-strict (32)). All four are necessary (evidence checks 1–3 for the first three; `h32` is the content).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.mainCoeffBound`, `Roth.two_lt_rpow_aux` (private), `Roth.sqrt_lt_floor`, `Roth.mul_log_div_two_lt_log_mainCoeffBound`
- **Used by**: Roth/MainLemma.lean:631 (`exists_mem_RSet_le_index_of_log_bound_aux`, second conjunct with `α := 0`), Roth/MainLemma.lean:656 (`exists_auxiliary_polynomial_of_log_bound_aux`, first and third conjuncts), Roth/Main.lean:502 (first conjunct). No other uses.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 171–212 (docstring 171–179, statement 180–184), proof length 28 lines
- **Notes**: none

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.three_small_factors.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.three_small_factors.md)_

#### `lemma Roth.exists_norm_coeff_reduction_aux`

### `theorem Roth.exists_norm_coeff_reduction_aux`
- **Type**: `∀ {R : Type*} [SeminormedRing R] {f P : Polynomial R} {A C : ℝ}, (∀ i, ‖f.coeff i‖ ≤ A) → (∀ i, ‖P.coeff i‖ ≤ C) → ∀ {s : ℕ}, P.natDegree ≤ s → f ≠ 1 → P %ₘ f ≠ P → 0 < f.natDegree ∧ f.natDegree ≤ s ∧ ∃ Q : Polynomial R, Q.natDegree ≤ s - 1 ∧ (∀ i, ‖Q.coeff i‖ ≤ (1 + A) * C) ∧ P %ₘ f = Q %ₘ f`
- **What**: One step of polynomial long division with coefficient-size control. If f has coefficients of norm at most A, P has coefficients of norm at most C and degree at most s, f is not 1 and reducing P modulo f actually changes P, then f is monic of degree between 1 and s, and subtracting the appropriate multiple f · lc(P) X^{deg P - deg f} gives a polynomial Q of degree at most s - 1 with coefficient norms at most (1 + A) C and the same remainder modulo f.
- **How**: From `hself` derives `Nontrivial R` (`Polynomial.Nontrivial.of_polynomial_ne`), monicity of f (contrapositive of `Polynomial.modByMonic_eq_of_not_monic`), `P ≠ 0`, and `f.degree ≤ P.degree` (contrapositive of `Polynomial.modByMonic_eq_self_iff`). Sets `Q := P - f * (C lc(P) * X^(deg P - deg f))`; `hmod` by unfolding `Polynomial.modByMonic` and one step of `Polynomial.divModByMonicAux` (`dite_eq_left`). Degree facts: `Polynomial.eq_one_of_monic_natDegree_zero`, `Polynomial.natDegree_le_natDegree`, and `Polynomial.div_wf_lemma` + `Polynomial.natDegree_lt_natDegree` for `deg Q < deg P`. Coefficient bound: `Polynomial.coeff_sub`, `Polynomial.coeff_mul_X_pow'`, `Polynomial.coeff_mul_C`, `norm_sub_le`, `norm_mul_le`, `mul_le_mul`, with `split_ifs` on the shifted index.
- **Hypotheses**: `[SeminormedRing R]` (no commutativity); coefficient bounds `hfA`, `hP`; `hs : P.natDegree ≤ s`; `hf1 : f ≠ 1`; `hself : P %ₘ f ≠ P` (encodes f monic and deg f ≤ deg P, P ≠ 0). Nonnegativity of A, C is derived from the norm bounds at index 0.
- **Uses from project**: `[]` (only mathlib lemmas)
- **Used by**: Roth/MainLemma.lean: `norm_coeff_modByMonic_le` (l.264, `obtain ⟨hn, hfs, Q, hQdeg, hQc, hmod⟩ := exists_norm_coeff_reduction_aux hfA hP hs hf1 hself`, the strong-induction step) only — `grep -rn exists_norm_coeff_reduction_aux Roth/` gives the definition and that use. Transitively everything downstream of `norm_coeff_modByMonic_le` (Lemma 9 remainder-coefficient bound in the main lemma).
- **Visibility**: private
- **Lines**: 212–246, proof length 31 lines
- **Notes**: Declared `private lemma` (kind recorded as theorem). The conclusions `0 < f.natDegree` and `f.natDegree ≤ s` are returned only to feed the `lia` arithmetic `s + 1 - deg f = (s - 1) + 1 - deg f + 1` in the caller. `hmod` could likely be proven more robustly by `Polynomial.modByMonic_eq_of_dvd_sub hf` (P - Q = f * (...) is divisible by f) rather than unfolding the internal `divModByMonicAux`; not tested here (style suggestion only).

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.exists_norm_coeff_reduction_aux.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.exists_norm_coeff_reduction_aux.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.norm_coeff_modByMonic_le`

### `theorem Roth.norm_coeff_modByMonic_le`
- **Type**: `∀ {R : Type*} [SeminormedRing R] {f : R[X]} {A : ℝ}, (∀ i, ‖f.coeff i‖ ≤ A) → ∀ (P : R[X]) {C : ℝ}, (∀ i, ‖P.coeff i‖ ≤ C) → ∀ {s : ℕ}, P.natDegree ≤ s → ∀ (i : ℕ), ‖(P %ₘ f).coeff i‖ ≤ (1 + A) ^ (s + 1 - f.natDegree) * C`
- **What**: If every coefficient of f has norm at most A, every coefficient of P has norm at most C, and deg P ≤ s, then every coefficient of the remainder P mod f (mathlib's `modByMonic`) has norm at most (1 + A)^(s + 1 − deg f) · C. No monicity is assumed (for nonmonic f, `modByMonic` returns P and the bound is trivial).
- **How**: Derives `1 ≤ 1 + A` from `hfA 0`; disposes of `f = 1` by `simpa` (remainder 0); then strong induction on `s` (`Nat.strong_induction_on`). If `P %ₘ f = P` the bound follows from `hP` and `one_le_pow₀`; otherwise the private helper `exists_norm_coeff_reduction_aux` supplies Q of degree ≤ s − 1, coefficient bound (1 + A)·C and `P %ₘ f = Q %ₘ f`, and the induction hypothesis plus `pow_succ`/`lia` closes it.
- **Hypotheses**: `[SeminormedRing R]` (no commutativity, no monicity); `hfA` coefficient bound for f; `hP` coefficient bound for P; `hs : P.natDegree ≤ s`. Nonnegativity of A, C is derived.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.exists_norm_coeff_reduction_aux` (private)

#### `def Roth.boxFamily`

### `def Roth.boxFamily`
- **Type**: `{σ : Type*} → (r : σ → ℕ) → (B : ℕ) → Set (MvPolynomial σ ℤ)`
- **What**: The set `𝒲` of integer polynomials `W` with `degreeOf j W ≤ r j` for every variable `j` and every coefficient in `[0, B]`.
- **How**: Set-builder `{W | (∀ j, degreeOf j W ≤ r j) ∧ ∀ d, 0 ≤ W.coeff d ∧ W.coeff d ≤ B}`.
- **Hypotheses**: none (no finiteness on `σ`; finiteness enters only in `boxFamily_finite`/`ncard_boxFamily` via `[Finite (Function.support r)]`).
- **Uses from project**: `[]` (only mathlib `MvPolynomial.degreeOf`, `MvPolynomial.coeff`)
- **Used by**: Roth/MainLemma.lean: private `boxFamily_eq_image_finsupp_aux` (l.281), `boxFamily_finite` (l.302), `ncard_boxFamily` (l.313), statements/proofs at l.361, 364, 367, 368, 373 (`pigeonhole_count`), l.389 (`exists_ne_modByMonic_eq`), l.552, l.601 (main Lemma 9 construction); module docstring l.35. No uses outside Roth/MainLemma.lean.

- **Lines**: 275–276, proof length 1 line (definition body)
- **Notes**: Source §6 proof of Lemma 9 (`.mathlib-quality/references/clean.tex` l.1405–1415, "`|𝒲| = (B_1+1)^r`"). The private aux lemma already rewrites the family as an image of `Finset.finsupp` over `Finset.Iic (Finsupp.ofSupportFinite r _)`.

#### `lemma Roth.boxFamily_eq_image_finsupp_aux`

### `theorem Roth.boxFamily_eq_image_finsupp_aux`
- **Type**: `∀ {σ : Type*} (r : σ → ℕ) [Finite (Function.support r)] (B : ℕ), boxFamily r B = AddMonoidAlgebra.ofCoeff '' ↑((Finset.Iic (Finsupp.ofSupportFinite r (Set.toFinite _))).finsupp fun _ ↦ Finset.Icc (0 : ℤ) B)` (declared `private lemma` under `open scoped Classical in`; the image lives in `Set ((σ →₀ ℕ) →₀ ℤ)` coerced to `MvPolynomial σ ℤ`).
- **What**: The box family (integer polynomials with `degreeOf j W ≤ r j` for all `j` and all coefficients in `[0, B]`) equals the set of polynomials whose coefficient function is a finitely supported map supported in the monomial box `{d : d ≤ r}` (as `Finset.Iic` of `r` viewed as a finsupp) with values in `Icc 0 B`; i.e. the box family is the image of the finite set `Finset.finsupp (Iic r) (fun _ ↦ Icc 0 B)` under the coefficient-to-polynomial map.
- **How**: `ext W`, two directions. (→) witness `W.coeff`; membership via `Finset.mem_finsupp_iff`, support condition from `MvPolynomial.monomial_le_degreeOf` composed with the degree bound and `Finset.mem_Iic`, value condition from `Finset.mem_Icc`. (←) from `Finset.mem_finsupp_iff` get support and value conditions; degree bound by `MvPolynomial.degreeOf_le_iff`; coefficient bound by case split on `d ∈ Iic r`, using `Finset.mem_Icc` inside and `Finsupp.notMem_support_iff` (coefficient zero) outside.
- **Hypotheses**: `[Finite (Function.support r)]` is needed to form `Finsupp.ofSupportFinite r` and hence the finite box `Finset.Iic`; without it `boxFamily r B` can be infinite (e.g. `σ = ℕ`, `r = 1`, `B = 1`), so no finset image exists. No other hypotheses. All used.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.boxFamily` (Roth/MainLemma.lean:275).
- **Used by**: Roth/MainLemma.lean only: `Roth.boxFamily_finite` (l.304, `rw`) and `Roth.ncard_boxFamily` (l.313, `rw` before `Set.ncard_image_of_injective` / `Finset.card_finsupp`). `grep -rn boxFamily_eq_image_finsupp_aux Roth/` returns only the declaration and these two uses.
- **Visibility**: private
- **Lines**: 280–298 (statement 280–283), proof length 15 lines (284–298)
- **Notes**: Name ends in `_aux` (would also trigger the Step 9a pre-filter). The preceding comment at l.278 is a plain `/- ... -/` comment, not a docstring, and its text ("The polynomial with coefficient `c d` at each monomial `d` of the box") describes the image map rather than the lemma. `classical` at l.284 is redundant given `open scoped Classical in` on the declaration (style nit only).

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.boxFamily_eq_image_finsupp_aux.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.boxFamily_eq_image_finsupp_aux.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.boxFamily_finite`

### `theorem Roth.boxFamily_finite`
- **Type**: `∀ {σ : Type*} (r : σ → ℕ) [Finite (Function.support r)] (B : ℕ), (boxFamily r B).Finite`
- **What**: The family `𝒲` of integer polynomials with `degreeOf j W ≤ r j` for all `j` and all coefficients in `[0, B]` is finite, provided only finitely many degree bounds `r j` are nonzero (σ may be infinite).
- **How**: Rewrites with the private `boxFamily_eq_image_finsupp_aux` (the family is the image under `AddMonoidAlgebra.ofCoeff` of `Finset.finsupp (Finset.Iic (Finsupp.ofSupportFinite r _)) (fun _ ↦ Finset.Icc 0 B)`), then closes with `(Finset.finite_toSet _).image _`.
- **Hypotheses**: `[Finite (Function.support r)]` (needed: with infinitely many positive `r j` the family is infinite).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.boxFamily`, `Roth.boxFamily_eq_image_finsupp_aux` (private)
- **Used by**: Roth/MainLemma.lean:367 (`exists_ne_modByMonic_eq`, to build `Fintype (boxFamily r B)`). No other uses in `Roth/`.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 299–305, proof length 3 lines
- **Notes**: Source §6, proof of Lemma 9 (`.mathlib-quality/references/clean.tex` l.1405–1415, where `|𝒲| = (B_1+1)^r` implicitly asserts finiteness). Sibling `ncard_boxFamily` (l.312) gives the exact count.

#### `theorem Roth.ncard_boxFamily`

### `theorem Roth.ncard_boxFamily`
- **Type**: `∀ {σ : Type*} (r : σ → ℕ) [Finite (Function.support r)] (B : ℕ), (boxFamily r B).ncard = (B + 1) ^ ∏ᶠ j, (r j + 1)`
- **What**: The family `𝒲` of integer polynomials with `degreeOf j W ≤ r j` and all coefficients in `[0, B]` has exactly `(B+1)^N` elements, `N = ∏ᶠ j, (r j + 1)` the number of monomials in the box `d ≤ r`.
- **How**: Rewrites with the private `boxFamily_eq_image_finsupp_aux` (image of `Finset.finsupp (Finset.Iic (ofSupportFinite r _)) (fun _ ↦ Icc 0 B)` under `AddMonoidAlgebra.ofCoeff`), then `Set.ncard_image_of_injective` with `AddMonoidAlgebra.ofCoeff_injective`, `Set.ncard_coe_finset`, `Finset.card_finsupp`; simplifies with `Int.card_Icc`, `Finsupp.card_Iic`, `Nat.card_Iic`, and converts the finset product to a `finprod` via `finprod_eq_prod_of_mulSupport_subset`.
- **Hypotheses**: `[Finite (Function.support r)]` (needed: otherwise the box of monomials is infinite and `ncard` is junk 0).
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.boxFamily`, `Roth.boxFamily_eq_image_finsupp_aux` (private)
- **Used by**: Roth/MainLemma.lean:373 (`exists_ne_modByMonic_eq`, in the `simpa` computing `Fintype.card` of the box family for the pigeonhole); mentioned in the module docstring l.43. No other uses in `Roth/`.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 311–318, proof length 6 lines
- **Notes**: Source §6, proof of Lemma 9 (`.mathlib-quality/references/clean.tex` l.1415, `|𝒲| = (B_1+1)^r`). Sibling of `boxFamily_finite`.

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.ncard_boxFamily.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.ncard_boxFamily.md)_

#### `lemma Roth.floor_rpow_count_lt_aux`

### `theorem Roth.floor_rpow_count_lt_aux`
- **Type**: `∀ {x c : ℝ}, 0 < x → 1 ≤ c → ((2 * ⌊x ^ c⌋₊ + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) < (x + 1) ^ (2 * c)`
- **What**: For real x > 0 and real exponent c ≥ 1, the number 2⌊x^c⌋ + 1 (the count of integers in [-⌊x^c⌋, ⌊x^c⌋]) is strictly less than (x + 1)^(2c).
- **How**: Set u = x^c > 0 (`Real.rpow_pos_of_pos`). Superadditivity of t ↦ t^c for c ≥ 1 (`Real.add_rpow_le_rpow_add`, Mathlib/Analysis/MeanInequalitiesPow.lean:190, with a = x, b = 1) gives u + 1 ≤ (x+1)^c. Rewrite (x+1)^(2c) = ((x+1)^c)^2 via `mul_comm`, `Real.rpow_mul` and `Real.rpow_two`; `push_cast`; then `nlinarith only` with `pow_le_pow_left₀` (squaring the superadditivity bound), `sq_pos_of_pos hu` and `Nat.floor_le`: 2⌊u⌋+1 ≤ 2u+1 < u^2+2u+1 = (u+1)^2 ≤ ((x+1)^c)^2.
- **Hypotheses**: `0 < x` (needed: at x = 0 the claim reads 1 < 1); `1 ≤ c` (needed: at c = 0 the claim reads 3 < 1, and superadditivity of t^c fails for c < 1).
- **Uses from project**: `[]` (only mathlib lemmas: `Real.rpow_pos_of_pos`, `Real.add_rpow_le_rpow_add`, `Real.rpow_mul`, `Real.rpow_two`, `pow_le_pow_left₀`, `sq_pos_of_pos`, `Nat.floor_le`).
- **Used by**: Roth/MainLemma.lean:343 `Roth.pigeonhole_count` (first `calc` step, via `pow_lt_pow_left₀`) only; `grep -rn floor_rpow_count_lt_aux Roth/` gives the definition (l.320) and that use. Transitively `pigeonhole_count` is used at Roth/MainLemma.lean:615 (Lemma 9 construction).
- **Visibility**: private
- **Lines**: 320–327, proof length 6 lines
- **Notes**: Declared `private lemma` (kind recorded as theorem). Name ends in `_aux`. Replaces the source's intermediate estimate `1 + 2B_1^c < [2(1+B_1)]^c` (§6, proof of Lemma 9) with a direct bound by (B+1)^(2c), as the comment in `pigeonhole_count` (l.336–338) explains.

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.floor_rpow_count_lt_aux.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.floor_rpow_count_lt_aux.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.pigeonhole_count`

### `theorem Roth.pigeonhole_count`
- **Type**: `∀ {B : ℝ}, 0 < B → ∀ {c : ℝ}, 1 ≤ c → ∀ {n D N : ℕ}, 0 < N → c * ↑n * ↑D ≤ ↑N / 2 → ((2 * ⌊B ^ c⌋₊ + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) ^ (n * D) < (B + 1) ^ N`
- **What**: For real `B > 0`, real `c ≥ 1`, naturals `n, D, N` with `N > 0` and `c n D ≤ N/2`, the number of remainder tuples `(2⌊B^c⌋ + 1)^{nD}` is strictly less than `(B + 1)^N` (the size of the box family when `B` is a natural number).
- **How**: Case `n * D = 0`: both sides reduce to `1 < (B+1)^N`, closed by `one_lt_pow₀`. Otherwise a three-step `calc`: `pow_lt_pow_left₀` applied to the private per-factor bound `floor_rpow_count_lt_aux` (`2⌊B^c⌋+1 < (B+1)^{2c}`), then `Real.rpow_mul_natCast` to merge exponents, then `Real.rpow_natCast` and `Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le` with `linarith only [hD]` for `2c·nD ≤ N`.
- **Hypotheses**: `hB : 0 < B` (necessary, fails at `B = 0`); `hc : 1 ≤ c` (cannot be dropped, fails at `c = 1/2`; not sharp, any valid threshold exceeds `log 3 / log 4`); `hN : 0 < N` (necessary, fails at `n = D = N = 0`); `hD : c n D ≤ N / 2` (necessary). All checked in the evidence file.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.floor_rpow_count_lt_aux` (private)
- **Used by**: Roth/MainLemma.lean:615 (private `exists_mem_RSet_le_index_of_rpow_bound_aux`, supplying the `hcount` argument of `exists_mem_RSet_le_index_aux`); listed in the module docstring l.43. No other uses in `Roth/`.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 329–351 (docstring 329–333, statement 334–335, proof 336–351, proof length 16 lines)
- **Notes**: Source clean.tex §6 l.1480–1500 (`(1+2B_1^c)^{nD} < [2(1+B_1)]^{cnD} ≤ [2(1+B_1)]^{r/2} < (1+B_1)^r`). The Lean proof bypasses the source's intermediate `Y = 2(1+B)` by bounding each factor by `(B+1)^{2c}` directly (comment l.337–339). Lean hypotheses (`B > 0`, `c ≥ 1`) are weaker than the source's (`B_1 > 1`, `c > 1`).

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.pigeonhole_count.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.pigeonhole_count.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.exists_ne_modByMonic_eq`

### `theorem Roth.exists_ne_modByMonic_eq`
- **Type**: `∀ {σ : Type u_1} (r : σ → ℕ) [Finite ↑(Function.support r)] (B : ℕ) {f : ℤ[X]}, f.Monic → ∀ (J : Finset (σ →₀ ℕ)) (C : ℕ), (∀ W ∈ boxFamily r B, ∀ j ∈ J, ∀ i < f.natDegree, |(diag (hasseDeriv j W) %ₘ f).coeff i| ≤ ↑C) → ↑(2 * C + 1) ^ (f.natDegree * J.card) < (↑B + 1) ^ ∏ᶠ j, (r j + 1) → ∃ W' ∈ boxFamily r B, ∃ W'' ∈ boxFamily r B, W' ≠ W'' ∧ ∀ j ∈ J, diag (hasseDeriv j W') %ₘ f = diag (hasseDeriv j W'') %ₘ f`
- **What**: If every member `W` of the box family `𝒲` (degree box `r`, coefficients in `[0, B]`) has all diagonal Hasse remainders `Δ_j W(x,…,x) mod f`, `j ∈ J`, with low coefficients bounded by `C`, and `(2C+1)^{deg f · |J|} < (B+1)^{∏(r_j+1)} = |𝒲|`, then two distinct `W', W'' ∈ 𝒲` have identical remainders for every `j ∈ J`.
- **How**: Builds a `Fintype` on `boxFamily r B` from `boxFamily_finite`, maps each `W` to its coefficient tuple `F W : J × Fin (deg f) → Icc (-C) C`, applies `Fintype.exists_ne_map_eq_of_card_lt` (the cardinality inequality from `hcount` via `Fintype.card_fun`, `Int.card_Icc`, `Set.fintypeCard_eq_ncard`, `ncard_boxFamily`), then recovers equality of remainders from equality of their low coefficients via `Polynomial.sum_modByMonic_coeff hf`.
- **Hypotheses**: `[Finite (Function.support r)]` (needed for `boxFamily_finite`/`ncard_boxFamily`); `hf : f.Monic` (needed: without it `%ₘ` is the identity and bounding coefficients below `natDegree f` does not determine the polynomial; e.g. one variable, `r = 1`, `B = 1`, `f = 2X`, `J = {0}`, `C = 1` satisfies `hC` and `3 < 4`, but the four `W` have distinct remainders); `hC`; `hcount`.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.boxFamily`, `Roth.boxFamily_finite`, `Roth.ncard_boxFamily`, `Roth.diag`, `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv`
- **Used by**: Roth/MainLemma.lean:557 (private `exists_mem_RSet_le_index_aux`); listed in the module docstring l.43. No other uses in `Roth/`.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 350–378 (docstring 350–358, statement 359–364, proof 365–378, proof length 14 lines)
- **Notes**: Source clean.tex §6 l.1501–1504 and the `\lean{}` note l.1514–1520 (finite pigeonhole on the coefficient arrays). Siblings: `Roth.pigeonhole_count` (BORDERLINE), `Roth.ncard_boxFamily` (YES-but-generalise-first), `Roth.boxFamily` (NO-composable).

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.exists_ne_modByMonic_eq.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.exists_ne_modByMonic_eq.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.sub_mem_RSet`

### `theorem Roth.sub_mem_RSet`
- **Type**: `∀ {σ : Type*} {r : σ → ℕ} {B : ℕ} {W' W'' : MvPolynomial σ ℤ}, W' ∈ Roth.boxFamily r B → W'' ∈ Roth.boxFamily r B → W' ≠ W'' → W' - W'' ∈ Roth.RSet (↑B) r`
- **What**: If `W'` and `W''` are distinct members of the family `𝒲` (degree at most `r j` in each variable `X j`, every coefficient in `[0, B]`), then `W' - W''` is nonzero, still has degree at most `r j` in each variable, and has every coefficient of absolute value at most `B`; that is, it lies in `ℛ(B; r)`.
- **How**: `refine` with three components: `sub_ne_zero.2 hne`; `(MvPolynomial.degreeOf_sub_le j W' W'').trans (max_le ..)`; and for coefficients `rw [MvPolynomial.coeff_sub, ← Int.cast_abs]` followed by `exact_mod_cast abs_sub_le_of_nonneg_of_le` applied to the four box bounds.
- **Hypotheses**: `h' : W' ∈ boxFamily r B`, `h'' : W'' ∈ boxFamily r B`, `hne : W' ≠ W''`. No finiteness of `σ` or of the support of `r`.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.boxFamily`, `Roth.RSet`
- **Used by**: `Roth.exists_mem_RSet_le_index_aux` (private, Roth/MainLemma.lean:560). No other uses in `Roth/`.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 388–394, proof length 4 lines
- **Notes**: Source clean.tex §6, proof of Lemma 9 (line 1511): `W^*` is not identically zero and its coefficients do not exceed `B_1` in absolute value.

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.sub_mem_RSet.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.sub_mem_RSet.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.eval_hasseDeriv_sub_eq_zero`

### `theorem Roth.eval_hasseDeriv_sub_eq_zero`
- **Type**: `∀ {σ : Type*} {S : Type*} [CommRing S] {f : Polynomial ℤ} {α : S}, Polynomial.aeval α f = 0 → ∀ {W' W'' : MvPolynomial σ ℤ} {j : σ →₀ ℕ}, Roth.diag (hasseDeriv j W') %ₘ f = Roth.diag (hasseDeriv j W'') %ₘ f → MvPolynomial.eval (fun _ ↦ α) (hasseDeriv j (MvPolynomial.map (Int.castRingHom S) (W' - W''))) = 0`
- **What**: If `f(α) = 0` and the diagonal substitutions `Δ_j W'(x, …, x)` and `Δ_j W''(x, …, x)` have the same remainder modulo `f`, then the Hasse derivative `Δ_j (W' - W'')`, with coefficients pushed into `S`, vanishes at the diagonal point `(α, …, α)`.
- **How**: (i) `f ∣ diag (hasseDeriv j (W' - W''))` via `Polynomial.dvd_modByMonic_iff_dvd.mp` and `simp [map_sub, diag_sub, Polynomial.sub_modByMonic, h]`; (ii) `Polynomial.aeval_eq_zero_of_dvd_aeval_eq_zero hdvd hfα`, transported to the goal by `simpa [hasseDeriv_map, eval_map, ← algebraMap_int_eq, ← aeval_def, diag, MvPolynomial.comp_aeval_apply, Polynomial.aeval_X]`.
- **Hypotheses**: `hfα : aeval α f = 0`; `h` congruence of remainders. No monicity of `f` (`%ₘ` is used, but `dvd_modByMonic_iff_dvd` holds for any `f`), no finiteness of `σ`.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.diag`, `Roth.diag_sub`, `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv`, `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv_map`
- **Used by**: `Roth.exists_mem_RSet_le_index_aux` (private, Roth/MainLemma.lean:567). No other uses in `Roth/`.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 396–411 (docstring 396–401, statement 402–405), proof length 6 lines
- **Notes**: Paper step: §6, proof of Lemma 9 ("`Δ_j W^*(x_1,…,x_m)` are zero when `x_1 = ⋯ = x_m = α`").

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.eval_hasseDeriv_sub_eq_zero.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.eval_hasseDeriv_sub_eq_zero.md)_

#### `theorem Roth.le_index_of_forall_eval_eq_zero`

### `theorem Roth.le_index_of_forall_eval_eq_zero`
- **Type**: `{σ : Type*} [Fintype σ] {P : MvPolynomial σ ℝ} (hP : P ≠ 0) {r : σ → ℕ} (hd : ∀ j, degreeOf j P ≤ r j) (α : σ → ℝ) {c : ℝ} (hvan : ∀ d, (∀ j, d j ≤ r j) → weight r d < c → eval α (hasseDeriv d P) = 0) : c ≤ index P α r`
- **What**: For a nonzero real polynomial `P` with `deg_{x_j} P ≤ r_j` for all `j`, if every Hasse derivative `Δ_d P` with `d` in the box `d ≤ r` and weight `∑ d_j/r_j < c` vanishes at `α`, then the index of `P` at `α` relative to `r` is at least `c`.
- **How**: `(le_index_iff hP).2`: for `d` with `(Δ_d P)(α) ≠ 0`, argue by contradiction on `weight r d < c`; `d` lies in the box because otherwise `d j > r j ≥ degreeOf j P` for some `j` and `hasseDeriv_eq_zero_of_degreeOf_lt` makes `Δ_d P = 0` (then `map_zero`); inside the box `hvan` gives vanishing, contradiction.
- **Hypotheses**: `hP : P ≠ 0` (needed by `le_index_iff`, junk `index 0 = 0`; necessary, evidence (2)); `hd` coordinate degree bounds (restrict the check to the box; necessary, evidence (3)); `hvan` the vanishing hypothesis. No positivity of `r`.
- **Uses from project**: `Roth.index`, `Roth.weight`, `Roth.le_index_iff`, `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv`, `MvPolynomial.hasseDeriv_eq_zero_of_degreeOf_lt`
- **Used by**: `Roth.exists_mem_RSet_le_index_aux` (private, Roth/MainLemma.lean:562). No other uses in `Roth/`.
- **Visibility**: public
- **Lines**: 412–425 (docstring 412–418, statement 419–422), proof length 3 lines
- **Notes**: Paper step: §6, proof of Lemma 9 (clean.tex l.1511, "Multi-indices outside the degree box give identically zero derivatives as well. Hence the index of W^* ... is at least γ").

_Detail report: [.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.le_index_of_forall_eval_eq_zero.md](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/Roth.le_index_of_forall_eval_eq_zero.md)_

| 7 | `MvPolynomial.hasseCoeff_eq_prod` | Roth/HasseDeriv.lean:106 | YES-but-generalise-first | CHEAP | KEEP | The full-product formula binom(k,d) = prod over all j of binom(k_j,d_j) is the literature definition of the multi-index binomial and the bridge from the support-indexed Finsupp.prod to Finset.prod API; absent from mathlib, it should ship with the parent Finsupp.choose in the mathlib idiom choose_eq_prod_of_support_subset (any finset s containing d.support, mirroring Finsupp.multinomial_eq_of_support_subset) plus the Fintype corollary choose_eq_prod. | [report](.mathlib-quality/overview/mathlibable/MvPolynomial.hasseCoeff_eq_prod.md) |
